```
104	## Mode: Split
105	
106	### Workflow
107	
108	1. The user loads an input PDF via a file picker dialog or by dragging and dropping a file onto the app window.
109	2. The app displays all pages as thumbnails in the left panel. Clicking a thumbnail selects it and updates the detail panel on the right.
110	3. The user defines split points by clicking in the gaps between page thumbnails. A visual divider appears at each split point; clicking again removes it. Dividers can also be repositioned by dragging them to a different gap. Each divider marks where a new output file begins.
111	4. The user sets a global filename template using `{date}` (today's date as `YYYY-MM-DD`) and `{name}` (a per-file label). Example: `{date} {name}` → `2026-06-12 invoice.pdf`. The app prefills the filename for each output file using this template. The user can then edit any individual file's name before exporting.
112	5. The user sets a global output folder (an existing folder on the local filesystem, or a Google Drive folder if Drive integration is enabled). For each output file, the user can adjust:
113	   - The prefilled filename (editable)
114	   - The destination folder (overridable per file)
115	6. The user clicks Export. Before splitting, the app checks for filename conflicts at each destination. If any conflict is found, the export is aborted and an error message identifies the conflicting files. Once resolved, the app splits the input PDF and writes (or uploads) each output file. Afterwards, the app prompts the user to keep, move, or delete the input file.
116	
117	### Layout
118	
119	Split mode uses a two-column layout:
120	
121	- **Left panel** (adjustable width, drag handle on right edge) — a single vertically scrolling area that combines the thumbnail strip and output file controls.
122	- **Right panel** (fills remaining space) — the page detail view.
123	
124	#### Left panel structure
125	
126	The left panel is a continuous scroll area. Pages are grouped by output file. Each group is preceded by a compact **output file header** containing the editable filename field and (when applicable) destination folder. Split-point dividers between groups are the visual boundary between one output file and the next.
127	
128	```
129	┌─ invoice.pdf ──────────────────────┐
130	│ folder: /Documents                 │
131	└────────────────────────────────────┘
132	  [page 1 thumbnail]
133	  [page 2 thumbnail]
134	  [page 3 thumbnail]
135	  ──────── [gap / split point] ───────
136	┌─ receipt.pdf ──────────────────────┐
137	│ folder: /Documents                 │
138	└────────────────────────────────────┘
139	  [page 4 thumbnail]
140	  [page 5 thumbnail]
141	```
142	
143	Clicking a gap toggles a split point there and creates a new output file section. The filename and folder fields for each section appear immediately above its pages.
144	
145	### Keyboard shortcuts
146	
147	| Key     | Action                                                  |
148	| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
149	| `Space` | Toggle a split point after the selected page            |
150	| `Tab`   | Move focus to the next filename input in the left panel |
151	
152	### Google Drive integration (optional)
153	
154	- Authentication uses Google OAuth via a browser window, triggered the first time Drive is enabled. Credentials are stored locally and reused in future sessions.
155	- When Drive is enabled, each output file header in the left panel shows a Drive folder field. Clicking it opens a folder browser modal that displays the user's Drive folder tree. The user navigates the tree and selects a destination folder. The selected path is shown in the header (e.g. `My Drive / Clients / Smith / 2026`).
156	- The folder tree is fetched lazily — the first time the user opens the folder browser in a session.
157	- The folder browser shows a **recently used folders** list at the top for quick access. This list persists across sessions.
158	- The global output folder setting can be set to a Drive folder, which prefills all output file headers. Individual headers can be overridden.
159	- After export, each output file is uploaded to its designated Drive folder. If an upload fails, the affected header shows an inline error message and a Retry button; other uploads are unaffected.
160	- When Drive is enabled, the local subfolder name for each output file is derived automatically from the Drive folder path (see Local folder sorting below).
161	
162	### Local folder sorting
163	
164	Each output file can be assigned a subfolder name. The file is then saved to `[output folder] / [subfolder] / filename.pdf` rather than directly into the output folder.
165	
166	When Drive is enabled, the subfolder name is derived automatically from the innermost component of the Drive folder path (e.g. `My Drive / Clients / Smith / 2026` → subfolder `2026`). When Drive is disabled, the user enters the subfolder name manually in the left panel header.
167	
168	If two or more output files in the same batch share the same subfolder name, the app prefixes the parent folder name to disambiguate. If the parent name is also shared, it walks further up the hierarchy until uniqueness is achieved. Examples:
169	
170	- `Clients / Smith / 2026` and `Clients / Jones / 2026` → `Smith - 2026` and `Jones - 2026`
171	- `Clients / Smith / 2026` and `Archive / Smith / 2026` → `Clients - Smith - 2026` and `Archive - Smith - 2026`
172	
173	(When Drive is disabled and names are entered manually, the user is responsible for avoiding conflicts — no automatic disambiguation applies.)
174	
175	To ensure disambiguation is fully deterministic, **the output folder must be empty when the input PDF is opened**. The app enforces this and shows an error if the folder is not empty.
176	
177	### Persisted settings
178	
179	The following settings are saved across sessions:
180	
181	- Last-used output folder
182	- Filename template
183	
184	### Error handling
185	
186	TBD.
```

1	package main
2	
3	import (
4		"fmt"
5		"io"
6		"os"
7		"path/filepath"
8		"slices"
9		"sort"
10		"strings"
11	
12		"github.com/pdfcpu/pdfcpu/pkg/api"
13	)
14	
15	// mergePDFs interleaves pages from pathA and pathB into outPath.
16	// If firstPageInA is true, file A contributes the odd-numbered output pages (1, 3, 5, …);
17	// otherwise file B does. If reverseB is true, file B's pages are reversed before interleaving,
18	// which is the typical case when the paper stack was flipped between scans.
19	// If the page counts differ, the extra pages from the longer file are
20	// appended in order after the interleaved section.
21	func mergePDFs(pathA, pathB, outPath string, firstPageInA, reverseB bool, skipA, skipB []int, rotationsA, rotationsB map[int]int) error {
22		tmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp("", "psp-merge-*")
23		if err != nil {
24			return err
25		}
26		defer os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)
27	
28		dirA := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "a")
29		dirB := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "b")
30		for _, d := range []string{dirA, dirB} {
31			if err := os.MkdirAll(d, 0o755); err != nil {
32				return err
33			}
34		}
35	
36		if err := api.SplitFile(pathA, dirA, 1, nil); err != nil {
37			return fmt.Errorf("splitting file A: %w", err)
38		}
39		if err := api.SplitFile(pathB, dirB, 1, nil); err != nil {
40			return fmt.Errorf("splitting file B: %w", err)
41		}
42	
43		pagesA, err := sortedPDFsInDir(dirA)
44		if err != nil {
45			return fmt.Errorf("listing file A pages: %w", err)
46		}
47		pagesB, err := sortedPDFsInDir(dirB)
48		if err != nil {
49			return fmt.Errorf("listing file B pages: %w", err)
50		}
51	
52		pagesA = filterSkipped(pagesA, skipA)
53		pagesB = filterSkipped(pagesB, skipB)
54		if reverseB {
55			slices.Reverse(pagesB)
56		}
57	
58		if err := applyRotations(pagesA, rotationsA); err != nil {
59			return fmt.Errorf("rotating file A pages: %w", err)
60		}
61		if err := applyRotations(pagesB, rotationsB); err != nil {
62			return fmt.Errorf("rotating file B pages: %w", err)
63		}
64	
65		if firstPageInA {
66			return api.MergeCreateFile(interleave(pagesA, pagesB), outPath, false, nil)
67		}
68		return api.MergeCreateFile(interleave(pagesB, pagesA), outPath, false, nil)
69	}
70	
71	func filterSkipped(pages []string, skip []int) []string {
72		if len(skip) == 0 {
73			return pages
74		}
75		skipSet := make(map[int]bool, len(skip))
76		for _, p := range skip {
77			skipSet[p] = true
78		}
79		out := make([]string, 0, len(pages))
80		for i, p := range pages {
81			if !skipSet[i+1] {
82				out = append(out, p)
83			}
84		}
85		return out
86	}
87	
88	// applyRotations rotates individual single-page PDF files in-place according to
89	// the rotations map (1-indexed page number → clockwise degrees: 90, 180, 270).
90	// The page number is derived from the pdfcpu split filename.
91	func applyRotations(pages []string, rotations map[int]int) error {
92		for _, p := range pages {
93			origPage := pdfFromPage(filepath.Base(p))
94			rot, ok := rotations[origPage]
95			if !ok || rot == 0 {
96				continue
97			}
98			tmp := p + ".rot.pdf"
99			if err := api.RotateFile(p, tmp, rot, nil, nil); err != nil {
100				return fmt.Errorf("page %d: %w", origPage, err)
101			}
102			if err := os.Rename(tmp, p); err != nil {
103				return err
104			}
105		}
106		return nil
107	}
108	
109	// splitPDF splits the PDF at inPath at the given page boundaries and writes
110	// each segment to outDir, returning the output file paths in order.
111	// splitAfter contains 1-indexed page numbers after which a new file begins.
112	// E.g. splitAfter=[2,4] on a 6-page PDF produces three files: pages 1-2, 3-4, 5-6.
113	func splitPDF(inPath string, splitAfter []int, outDir string) ([]string, error) {
114		if len(splitAfter) == 0 {
115			outPath := filepath.Join(outDir, filepath.Base(inPath))
116			if err := copyFile(inPath, outPath); err != nil {
117				return nil, err
118			}
119			return []string{outPath}, nil
120		}
121	
122		// SplitByPageNrFile expects the first page of each new segment.
123		pageNrs := make([]int, len(splitAfter))
124		for i, p := range splitAfter {
125			pageNrs[i] = p + 1
126		}
127	
128		if err := api.SplitByPageNrFile(inPath, outDir, pageNrs, nil); err != nil {
129			return nil, fmt.Errorf("splitting PDF: %w", err)
130		}
131	
132		return sortedPDFsInDir(outDir)
133	}
134	
135	// pdfPageCount returns the number of pages in the PDF at path.
136	func pdfPageCount(path string) (int, error) {
137		return api.PageCountFile(path)
138	}
139	
140	// sortedPDFsInDir lists PDF files in dir sorted numerically by the first page
141	// number encoded in pdfcpu's output filenames (e.g. "doc_3-5.pdf" → 3).
142	func sortedPDFsInDir(dir string) ([]string, error) {
143		entries, err := os.ReadDir(dir)
144		if err != nil {
145			return nil, err
146		}
147		type item struct {
148			path     string
149			fromPage int
150		}
151		var files []item
152		for _, e := range entries {
153			if e.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(strings.ToLower(e.Name()), ".pdf") {
154				continue
155			}
156			files = append(files, item{
157				path:     filepath.Join(dir, e.Name()),
158				fromPage: pdfFromPage(e.Name()),
159			})
160		}
161		sort.Slice(files, func(i, j int) bool { return files[i].fromPage < files[j].fromPage })
162		result := make([]string, len(files))
163		for i, f := range files {
164			result[i] = f.path
165		}
166		return result, nil
167	}
168	
169	// interleave merges two slices by alternating elements: a[0], b[0], a[1], b[1], ...
170	// If the slices have different lengths, the remaining elements of the longer
171	// slice are appended in order after the interleaved section.
172	func interleave[T any](a, b []T) []T {
173		n := min(len(a), len(b))
174		out := make([]T, 0, len(a)+len(b))
175		for i := range n {
176			out = append(out, a[i], b[i])
177		}
178		out = append(out, a[n:]...)
179		out = append(out, b[n:]...)
180		return out
181	}
182	
183	func copyFile(src, dst string) error {
184		in, err := os.Open(src)
185		if err != nil {
186			return err
187		}
188		defer in.Close()
189		out, err := os.Create(dst)
190		if err != nil {
191			return err
192		}
193		defer out.Close()
194		_, err = io.Copy(out, in)
195		return err
196	}
197	
198	// pdfFromPage extracts the first page number from a pdfcpu split filename
199	// such as "doc_1.pdf" or "doc_3-5.pdf".
200	func pdfFromPage(name string) int {
201		name = strings.TrimSuffix(name, ".pdf")
202		parts := strings.Split(name, "_")
203		if len(parts) == 0 {
204			return 0
205		}
206		rangeStr := parts[len(parts)-1]
207		n := 0
208		fmt.Sscanf(strings.SplitN(rangeStr, "-", 2)[0], "%d", &n)
209		return n
210	}
211

1	package main
2	
3	import (
4		"context"
5		"encoding/base64"
6		"fmt"
7		"os"
8		"os/exec"
9		"path/filepath"
10		"strconv"
11		"strings"
12	
13		"github.com/wailsapp/wails/v2/pkg/runtime"
14	)
15	
16	// App struct
17	type App struct {
18		ctx context.Context
19	}
20	
21	// NewApp creates a new App application struct
22	func NewApp() *App {
23		return &App{}
24	}
25	
26	// startup is called when the app starts. The context is saved
27	// so we can call the runtime methods
28	func (a *App) startup(ctx context.Context) {
29		a.ctx = ctx
30	}
31	
32	// OpenPDF shows a file-open dialog filtered to PDFs and returns the selected path.
33	// Returns an empty string if the user cancels.
34	func (a *App) OpenPDF() (string, error) {
35		path, err := runtime.OpenFileDialog(a.ctx, runtime.OpenDialogOptions{
36			Title: "Open PDF",
37			Filters: []runtime.FileFilter{
38				{DisplayName: "PDF Files (*.pdf)", Pattern: "*.pdf"},
39			},
40		})
41		return path, err
42	}
43	
44	// PageCount returns the number of pages in the PDF at path.
45	func (a *App) PageCount(path string) (int, error) {
46		return pdfPageCount(path)
47	}
48	
49	// RenderPage renders a single page of the PDF at path as a PNG and returns it
50	// base64-encoded. widthPx controls the output width; height is scaled proportionally.
51	func (a *App) RenderPage(path string, pageNum int, widthPx int) (string, error) {
52		tmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp("", "psp-render-*")
53		if err != nil {
54			return "", err
55		}
56		defer os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)
57	
58		outPrefix := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "page")
59		page := strconv.Itoa(pageNum)
60	
61		out, err := exec.Command(
62			"pdftoppm",
63			"-f", page,
64			"-l", page,
65			"-singlefile",
66			"-png",
67			"-scale-to-x", strconv.Itoa(widthPx),
68			"-scale-to-y", "-1",
69			path,
70			outPrefix,
71		).CombinedOutput()
72		if err != nil {
73			return "", fmt.Errorf("pdftoppm: %w\n%s", err, out)
74		}
75	
76		data, err := os.ReadFile(outPrefix + ".png")
77		if err != nil {
78			return "", err
79		}
80		return base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(data), nil
81	}
82	
83	// SavePDF shows a save-file dialog filtered to PDFs and returns the chosen path.
84	// Returns an empty string if the user cancels.
85	func (a *App) SavePDF() (string, error) {
86		path, err := runtime.SaveFileDialog(a.ctx, runtime.SaveDialogOptions{
87			Title:           "Save Merged PDF",
88			DefaultFilename: "merged.pdf",
89			Filters: []runtime.FileFilter{
90				{DisplayName: "PDF Files (*.pdf)", Pattern: "*.pdf"},
91			},
92		})
93		if err != nil || path == "" {
94			return path, err
95		}
96		if !strings.HasSuffix(strings.ToLower(path), ".pdf") {
97			path += ".pdf"
98		}
99		return path, nil
100	}
101	
102	// OpenFile opens the file at path with the system default application.
103	func (a *App) OpenFile(path string) error {
104		return exec.Command("open", path).Run()
105	}
106	
107	// MergePDFs interleaves pages from pathA and pathB and writes the result to outPath.
108	func (a *App) MergePDFs(pathA, pathB, outPath string, firstPageInA, reverseB bool, skipA, skipB []int, rotationsA, rotationsB map[int]int) error {
109		return mergePDFs(pathA, pathB, outPath, firstPageInA, reverseB, skipA, skipB, rotationsA, rotationsB)
110	}
111

10	function basename(p: string) {
11	  return p.split(/[\\/]/).pop() ?? p

15	  onOpenInSplitMode: (path: string) => void
16	}

18	export default function MergeMode({ onOpenInSplitMode }: Props) {
19	  const fileA = usePDFFile()
20	  const fileB = usePDFFile()

22	  const [firstPageIn, setFirstPageIn] = useState<FirstPageIn>('a')
23	  const [reverseB, setReverseB] = useState(true)
24	  const [merging, setMerging] = useState(false)

26	  const [totalWidth, setTotalWidth] = useState(DEFAULT_TOTAL_WIDTH)
27	
28	  useEffect(() => {
29	    return () => {
30	      if (fileA.path) pageCache.evict(fileA.path)
31	      if (fileB.path) pageCache.evict(fileB.path)
32	    }
33	  }, [fileA.path, fileB.path])
34	
35	  // Subtract 22px to account for scrollbar + gap
36	  const colWidth = Math.floor((totalWidth - 22) / 2)
37	
38	  const handleChoose = async (file: FirstPageIn) => {
39	    const loaded = await (file === 'a' ? fileA : fileB).load()
40	    if (loaded) setSelectedPage({ file, page: 1 })
41	  }
42	
43	  const handleMerge = async () => {
44	    if (!fileA.path || !fileB.path) return
45	    const outPath = await SavePDF()
46	    if (!outPath) return
47	    setMerging(true)
48	    try {
49	      await MergePDFs(

52	        [...fileA.skipped], [...fileB.skipped],
53	        Object.fromEntries(fileA.rotations), Object.fromEntries(fileB.rotations),
54	      )
55	      setMergedPath(outPath)
56	    } catch (e) {
57	      notifications.show({ title: 'Merge failed', message: String(e), color: 'red' })
58	    } finally {
59	      setMerging(false)
60	    }

92	        <FilePickerColumn label="File B" path={fileB.path} width={colWidth + 26} onChoose={() => handleChoose('b')} />
93	        <Group gap={8} px={12} style={{ flex: 1, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
94	          {unequalCounts && (
95	            <Tooltip
96	              label={`File A has ${fileA.count} page${fileA.count !== 1 ? 's' : ''}, File B has ${fileB.count} page${fileB.count !== 1 ? 's' : ''}. The extra ${Math.abs(fileA.count - fileB.count)} page${Math.abs(fileA.count - fileB.count) !== 1 ? 's' : ''} will be appended at the end.`}
97	              multiline
98	              w={280}
99	            >
100	              <IconAlertTriangle size={18} color="var(--mantine-color-yellow-6)" />
101	            </Tooltip>
102	          )}
103	          <Checkbox
104	            size="sm"
105	            label="Reverse File B"
106	            checked={reverseB}
107	            onChange={(e) => setReverseB(e.currentTarget.checked)}
108	          />
109	          <Text size="sm" c="dimmed">First page in</Text>
110	          <SegmentedControl
111	            size="xs"
112	            value={firstPageIn}
113	            onChange={(v) => setFirstPageIn(v as FirstPageIn)}
114	            data={[
115	              { label: 'File A', value: 'a' },
116	              { label: 'File B', value: 'b' },
117	            ]}
118	          />
119	          <Button size="sm" disabled={!bothLoaded} loading={merging} onClick={handleMerge}>

{

  "content": "import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react'\nimport { Box, Button, Group } from '@mantine/core'\nimport { notifications } from '@mantine/notifications'\nimport ThumbnailPanel from './ThumbnailPanel'\nimport DetailPanel from '../DetailPanel'\nimport { OpenPDF, PageCount, PickFolder, ExportSplit } from '../../../wailsjs/go/main/App'\n\ninterface Props {\n  initialPath?: string | null\n}\n\nexport default function SplitMode({ initialPath }: Props) {\n  const [pdfPath, setPdfPath] = useState<string | null>(null)\n  const [pageCount, setPageCount] = useState(0)\n  const [selectedPage, setSelectedPage] = useState(1)\n  const [splitPoints, setSplitPoints] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set())\n  const [exporting, setExporting] = useState(false)\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!initialPath) return\n    PageCount(initialPath)\n      .then(count => {\n        setPdfPath(initialPath)\n        setPageCount(count)\n        setSelectedPage(1)\n        setSplitPoints(new Set())\n      })\n      .catch(e => notifications.show({ title: 'Failed to open file', message: String(e), color: 'red' }))\n  }, [initialPath])\n\n  const handleOpen = async () => {\n    const path = await OpenPDF()\n    if (!path) return\n    try {\n      const count = await PageCount(path)\n      setPdfPath(path)\n      setPageCount(count)\n      setSelectedPage(1)\n      setSplitPoints(new Set())\n    } catch (e) {\n      notifications.show({ title: 'Failed to open file', message: String(e), color: 'red' })\n    }\n  }\n\n  const handleExport = async () => {\n    if (!pdfPath) return\n    const outDir = await PickFolder()\n    if (!outDir) return\n    setExporting(true)\n    try {\n      const sorted = [...splitPoints].sort((a, b) => a - b)\n      await ExportSplit(pdfPath, sorted, outDir)\n      const fileCount = splitPoints.size + 1\n      notifications.show({\n        title: 'Export complete',\n        message: `${fileCount} file${fileCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''} saved to ${outDir}`,\n        color: 'green',\n      })\n    } catch (e) {\n      notifications.show({ title: 'Export failed', message: String(e), color: 'red' })\n    } finally {\n      setExporting(false)\n    }\n  }\n\n  const toggleSplitPoint = useCallback((afterPage: number) => {\n    setSplitPoints(prev => {\n      const next = new Set(prev)\n      if (next.has(afterPage)) {\n        next.delete(afterPage)\n      } else {\n        next.add(afterPage)\n      }\n      return next\n    })\n  }, [])\n\n  return (\n    <Box style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100%' }}>\n      <Box\n        style={{\n          flexShrink: 0,\n          borderBottom: '1px solid var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',\n          display: 'flex',\n          alignItems: 'center',\n          paddingInline: 12,\n          height: 44,\n        }}\n      >\n        <Group gap={8} style={{ width: '100%', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>\n          <Button size=\"xs\" variant=\"default\" onClick={handleOpen}>\n            Open PDF\n          </Button>\n          <Button\n            size=\"xs\"\n            disabled={!pdfPath}\n            loading={exporting}\n            onClick={handleExport}\n          >\n            Export\n          </Button>\n        </Group>\n      </Box>\n\n      <Box style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'hidden', display: 'flex' }}>\n        {pdfPath ? (\n          <>\n            <ThumbnailPanel\n              pdfPath={pdfPath}\n              pageCount={pageCount}\n              selectedPage={selectedPage}\n              onSelectPage={setSelectedPage}\n              splitPoints={splitPoints}\n              onToggleSplitPoint={toggleSplitPoint}\n            />\n            <DetailPanel\n              pdfPath={pdfPath}\n              pageNum={selectedPage}\n              pageCount={pageCount}\n              onNavigate={setSelectedPage}\n            />\n          </>\n        ) : (\n          <Box style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>\n            <Button onClick={handleOpen}>Open PDF</Button>\n          </Box>\n        )}\n      </Box>\n    </Box>\n  )\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Done. Two commits:

- `cf9b87f` — `PickFolder` and `ExportSplit` RPCs in Go; toolbar with Open/Export buttons in SplitMode
- `bb0a5fa` — spec checked off

The Export button is disabled until a PDF is open. Clicking it opens a folder picker, splits the PDF at the defined split points, and shows a green notification with the file count on success.

> DEVELOPER

How are the filenames determined right now?

> AGENT

Tracing through the code:

- `handleExport` in `SplitMode/index.tsx` calls `ExportSplit(pdfPath, sorted, outDir)`
- `ExportSplit` in `app.go` calls `splitPDF(inPath, splitAfter, outDir)` from `pdf.go`
- In `splitPDF`:
  - **No split points**: copies the file with `filepath.Base(inPath)` — same name as the input file
  - **With split points**: calls `api.SplitByPageNrFile` from pdfcpu, which auto-generates names like `inputname_1-3.pdf`, `inputname_4-6.pdf` based on the page ranges

So right now filenames are entirely pdfcpu-derived from the input filename. Step 3 will replace this with per-section editable name fields.

1	import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react'
2	import { Box, Button, Group } from '@mantine/core'
3	import { notifications } from '@mantine/notifications'
4	import ThumbnailPanel from './ThumbnailPanel'
5	import DetailPanel from '../DetailPanel'
6	import { OpenPDF, PageCount, PickFolder, ExportSplit } from '../../../wailsjs/go/main/App'
7	
8	interface Props {
9	  initialPath?: string | null
10	}
11	
12	export default function SplitMode({ initialPath }: Props) {
13	  const [pdfPath, setPdfPath] = useState<string | null>(null)
14	  const [pageCount, setPageCount] = useState(0)
15	  const [selectedPage, setSelectedPage] = useState(1)
16	  const [splitPoints, setSplitPoints] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set())
17	  const [exporting, setExporting] = useState(false)
18	
19	  useEffect(() => {
20	    if (!initialPath) return
21	    PageCount(initialPath)
22	      .then(count => {
23	        setPdfPath(initialPath)
24	        setPageCount(count)
25	        setSelectedPage(1)
26	        setSplitPoints(new Set())
27	      })
28	      .catch(e => notifications.show({ title: 'Failed to open file', message: String(e), color: 'red' }))
29	  }, [initialPath])
30	
31	  const handleOpen = async () => {
32	    const path = await OpenPDF()
33	    if (!path) return
34	    try {
35	      const count = await PageCount(path)
36	      setPdfPath(path)
37	      setPageCount(count)
38	      setSelectedPage(1)
39	      setSplitPoints(new Set())
40	    } catch (e) {
41	      notifications.show({ title: 'Failed to open file', message: String(e), color: 'red' })
42	    }
43	  }
44	
45	  const handleExport = async () => {
46	    if (!pdfPath) return
47	    const outDir = await PickFolder()
48	    if (!outDir) return
49	    setExporting(true)
50	    try {
51	      const sorted = [...splitPoints].sort((a, b) => a - b)
52	      await ExportSplit(pdfPath, sorted, outDir)
53	      const fileCount = splitPoints.size + 1
54	      notifications.show({
55	        title: 'Export complete',
56	        message: `${fileCount} file${fileCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''} saved to ${outDir}`,
57	        color: 'green',
58	      })
59	    } catch (e) {
60	      notifications.show({ title: 'Export failed', message: String(e), color: 'red' })
61	    } finally {
62	      setExporting(false)
63	    }
64	  }
65	
66	  const toggleSplitPoint = useCallback((afterPage: number) => {
67	    setSplitPoints(prev => {
68	      const next = new Set(prev)
69	      if (next.has(afterPage)) {
70	        next.delete(afterPage)
71	      } else {
72	        next.add(afterPage)
73	      }
74	      return next
75	    })
76	  }, [])
77	
78	  return (
79	    <Box style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100%' }}>
80	      <Box
81	        style={{
82	          flexShrink: 0,
83	          borderBottom: '1px solid var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',
84	          display: 'flex',
85	          alignItems: 'center',
86	          paddingInline: 12,
87	          height: 44,
88	        }}
89	      >
90	        <Group gap={8} style={{ width: '100%', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
91	          <Button size="xs" variant="default" onClick={handleOpen}>
92	            Open PDF
93	          </Button>
94	          <Button
95	            size="xs"
96	            disabled={!pdfPath}
97	            loading={exporting}
98	            onClick={handleExport}
99	          >
100	            Export
101	          </Button>
102	        </Group>
103	      </Box>
104	
105	      <Box style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'hidden', display: 'flex' }}>
106	        {pdfPath ? (
107	          <>
108	            <ThumbnailPanel
109	              pdfPath={pdfPath}
110	              pageCount={pageCount}
111	              selectedPage={selectedPage}
112	              onSelectPage={setSelectedPage}
113	              splitPoints={splitPoints}
114	              onToggleSplitPoint={toggleSplitPoint}
115	            />
116	            <DetailPanel
117	              pdfPath={pdfPath}
118	              pageNum={selectedPage}
119	              pageCount={pageCount}
120	              onNavigate={setSelectedPage}
121	            />
122	          </>
123	        ) : (
124	          <Box style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
125	            <Button onClick={handleOpen}>Open PDF</Button>
126	          </Box>
127	        )}
128	      </Box>
129	    </Box>
130	  )
131	}
132

1	import { useRef, useState, useEffect } from 'react'
2	import { useVirtualizer } from '@tanstack/react-virtual'
3	import { Loader } from '@mantine/core'
4	import * as pageCache from '../../hooks/pageCache'
5	import { DEFAULT_WIDTH, DRAG_HANDLE_WIDTH, ITEM_PADDING, PAGE_ASPECT, LABEL_HEIGHT } from '../../constants'
6	
7	const MIN_WIDTH = 120
8	const MAX_WIDTH = 480
9	const GAP_HEIGHT = 16
10	
11	interface Props {
12	  pdfPath: string
13	  pageCount: number
14	  selectedPage: number
15	  onSelectPage: (page: number) => void
16	  splitPoints: Set<number>
17	  onToggleSplitPoint: (afterPage: number) => void
18	}
19	
20	export default function SplitThumbnailPanel({
21	  pdfPath, pageCount, selectedPage, onSelectPage, splitPoints, onToggleSplitPoint,
22	}: Props) {
23	  const [panelWidth, setPanelWidth] = useState(DEFAULT_WIDTH)
24	  const [hoveredGap, setHoveredGap] = useState<number | null>(null)
25	
26	  const thumbWidth = panelWidth - ITEM_PADDING * 2
27	  const thumbHeight = Math.round(thumbWidth * PAGE_ASPECT)
28	  const itemHeight = thumbHeight + LABEL_HEIGHT + ITEM_PADDING + GAP_HEIGHT
29	
30	  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
31	
32	  const virtualizer = useVirtualizer({
33	    count: pageCount,
34	    getScrollElement: () => scrollRef.current,
35	    estimateSize: () => itemHeight,
36	    overscan: 3,
37	  })
38	
39	  useEffect(() => {
40	    virtualizer.measure()
41	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
42	  }, [itemHeight])
43	
44	  const virtualItems = virtualizer.getVirtualItems()
45	  pageCache.usePageCacheRender()
46	
47	  useEffect(() => {
48	    for (const item of virtualItems) pageCache.load(pdfPath, item.index + 1, thumbWidth)
49	  })
50	
51	  useEffect(() => {
52	    virtualizer.scrollToIndex(selectedPage - 1, { align: 'auto' })
53	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
54	  }, [selectedPage])
55	
56	  useEffect(() => {
57	    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
58	      if (e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement || e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) return
59	      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
60	        e.preventDefault()
61	        if (selectedPage > 1) onSelectPage(selectedPage - 1)
62	      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
63	        e.preventDefault()
64	        if (selectedPage < pageCount) onSelectPage(selectedPage + 1)
65	      }
66	    }
67	    window.addEventListener('keydown', handler)
68	    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handler)
69	  }, [selectedPage, pageCount, onSelectPage])
70	
71	  const startDrag = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
72	    const startX = e.clientX
73	    const startWidth = panelWidth
74	    const clamp = (w: number) => Math.max(MIN_WIDTH, Math.min(MAX_WIDTH, w))
75	    const onMove = (ev: MouseEvent) => setPanelWidth(clamp(startWidth + ev.clientX - startX))
76	    const onUp = (ev: MouseEvent) => {
77	      setPanelWidth(clamp(startWidth + ev.clientX - startX))
78	      document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove)
79	      document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp)
80	    }
81	    document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove)
82	    document.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp)
83	    e.preventDefault()
84	  }
85	
86	  return (
87	    <div style={{ display: 'flex', height: '100%', flexShrink: 0 }}>
88	      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', width: panelWidth, height: '100%' }}>
89	        <div
90	          ref={scrollRef}
91	          style={{
92	            flex: 1,
93	            minHeight: 0,
94	            overflowY: 'auto',
95	            overflowX: 'hidden',
96	            background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',
97	          }}
98	        >
99	          <div style={{ height: virtualizer.getTotalSize(), position: 'relative' }}>
100	            {virtualItems.map(item => {
101	              const page = item.index + 1
102	              const src = pageCache.getSrc(pdfPath, page)
103	              const isSelected = page === selectedPage
104	              const isSplit = splitPoints.has(page)
105	              const isLastPage = page === pageCount
106	
107	              return (
108	                <div
109	                  key={item.key}
110	                  style={{
111	                    position: 'absolute',
112	                    top: item.start,
113	                    left: 0,
114	                    width: '100%',
115	                    height: item.size,
116	                    boxSizing: 'border-box',
117	                  }}
118	                >
119	                  {/* Thumbnail */}
120	                  <div
121	                    style={{ padding: ITEM_PADDING, paddingBottom: 0, cursor: 'pointer' }}
122	                    onClick={() => onSelectPage(page)}
123	                  >
124	                    <div
125	                      style={{
126	                        border: `2px solid ${isSelected ? 'var(--mantine-color-blue-5)' : 'transparent'}`,
127	                        borderRadius: 4,
128	                        overflow: 'hidden',
129	                        background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-1)',
130	                      }}
131	                    >
132	                      {src ? (
133	                        <img
134	                          src={src}
135	                          alt={`Page ${page}`}
136	                          style={{ width: '100%', display: 'block' }}
137	                          draggable={false}
138	                        />
139	                      ) : (
140	                        <div
141	                          style={{
142	                            width: '100%',
143	                            height: thumbHeight,
144	                            display: 'flex',
145	                            alignItems: 'center',
146	                            justifyContent: 'center',
147	                          }}
148	                        >
149	                          {pageCache.isLoading(pdfPath, page) && <Loader size="xs" />}
150	                        </div>
151	                      )}
152	                    </div>
153	                    <div
154	                      style={{
155	                        textAlign: 'center',
156	                        fontSize: 11,
157	                        color: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-7)',
158	                        height: LABEL_HEIGHT,
159	                        lineHeight: `${LABEL_HEIGHT}px`,
160	                      }}
161	                    >
162	                      {page}
163	                    </div>
164	                  </div>
165	
166	                  {/* Gap zone between thumbnails */}
167	                  {!isLastPage && (
168	                    <GapZone
169	                      isSplit={isSplit}
170	                      isHovered={hoveredGap === page}
171	                      onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onToggleSplitPoint(page) }}
172	                      onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredGap(page)}
173	                      onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredGap(null)}
174	                    />
175	                  )}
176	                </div>
177	              )
178	            })}
179	          </div>
180	        </div>
181	      </div>
182	
183	      <div
184	        onMouseDown={startDrag}
185	        style={{
186	          width: DRAG_HANDLE_WIDTH,
187	          height: '100%',
188	          cursor: 'col-resize',
189	          flexShrink: 0,
190	          background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',
191	        }}
192	      />
193	    </div>
194	  )
195	}
196	
197	interface GapZoneProps {
198	  isSplit: boolean
199	  isHovered: boolean
200	  onClick: (e: React.MouseEvent) => void
201	  onMouseEnter: () => void
202	  onMouseLeave: () => void
203	}
204	
205	function GapZone({ isSplit, isHovered, onClick, onMouseEnter, onMouseLeave }: GapZoneProps) {
206	  const bg = isSplit
207	    ? 'var(--mantine-color-blue-0)'
208	    : isHovered
209	    ? 'var(--mantine-color-gray-2)'
210	    : undefined
211	
212	  return (
213	    <div
214	      onClick={onClick}
215	      onMouseEnter={onMouseEnter}
216	      onMouseLeave={onMouseLeave}
217	      style={{
218	        height: GAP_HEIGHT,
219	        background: bg,
220	        display: 'flex',
221	        alignItems: 'center',
222	        paddingLeft: ITEM_PADDING,
223	        paddingRight: ITEM_PADDING,
224	        cursor: 'pointer',
225	      }}
226	    >
227	      {isSplit && (
228	        <div style={{ flex: 1, height: 2, background: 'var(--mantine-color-blue-5)', borderRadius: 1 }} />
229	      )}
230	      {!isSplit && isHovered && (
231	        <div style={{ flex: 1, height: 0, borderTop: '1px dashed var(--mantine-color-gray-5)' }} />
232	      )}
233	    </div>
234	  )
235	}

{

  "old_string": "// ExportSplit splits the PDF at inPath at the given page boundaries and writes\n// each segment to outDir. splitAfter contains 1-indexed page numbers after\n// which a new file begins.\nfunc (a *App) ExportSplit(inPath string, splitAfter []int, outDir string) error {\n\t_, err := splitPDF(inPath, splitAfter, outDir)\n\treturn err\n}",
  "new_string": "// ExportSplit splits the PDF at inPath at the given page boundaries and writes\n// each segment to outDir using the corresponding name from outNames (without\n// extension). An empty name falls back to \"output-N\". splitAfter contains\n// 1-indexed page numbers after which a new file begins.\nfunc (a *App) ExportSplit(inPath string, splitAfter []int, outDir string, outNames []string) error {\n\ttmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp(\"\", \"psp-split-*\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tdefer os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)\n\n\tpaths, err := splitPDF(inPath, splitAfter, tmpDir)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tfor i, src := range paths {\n\t\tname := \"\"\n\t\tif i < len(outNames) {\n\t\t\tname = strings.TrimSpace(outNames[i])\n\t\t}\n\t\tif name == \"\" {\n\t\t\tname = fmt.Sprintf(\"output-%d\", i+1)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !strings.HasSuffix(strings.ToLower(name), \".pdf\") {\n\t\t\tname += \".pdf\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := copyFile(src, filepath.Join(outDir, name)); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ben/git/paper-scan-processor/frontend/src/components/SplitMode/ThumbnailPanel.tsx",
  "content": "import { useRef, useState, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react'\nimport { useVirtualizer } from '@tanstack/react-virtual'\nimport { Loader } from '@mantine/core'\nimport * as pageCache from '../../hooks/pageCache'\nimport { DEFAULT_WIDTH, DRAG_HANDLE_WIDTH, ITEM_PADDING, PAGE_ASPECT, LABEL_HEIGHT } from '../../constants'\n\nconst MIN_WIDTH = 120\nconst MAX_WIDTH = 480\nconst GAP_HEIGHT = 16\nconst HEADER_HEIGHT = 44\n\ntype ListItem =\n  | { type: 'header'; fileIndex: number; firstPage: number }\n  | { type: 'page'; page: number }\n\nfunction buildItems(pageCount: number, splitPoints: Set<number>): ListItem[] {\n  const result: ListItem[] = []\n  let fileIndex = 0\n  for (let page = 1; page <= pageCount; page++) {\n    if (page === 1 || splitPoints.has(page - 1)) {\n      result.push({ type: 'header', fileIndex: fileIndex++, firstPage: page })\n    }\n    result.push({ type: 'page', page })\n  }\n  return result\n}\n\ninterface Props {\n  pdfPath: string\n  pageCount: number\n  selectedPage: number\n  onSelectPage: (page: number) => void\n  splitPoints: Set<number>\n  onToggleSplitPoint: (afterPage: number) => void\n  fileNames: Map<number, string>\n  onFileNameChange: (firstPage: number, name: string) => void\n}\n\nexport default function SplitThumbnailPanel({\n  pdfPath, pageCount, selectedPage, onSelectPage,\n  splitPoints, onToggleSplitPoint,\n  fileNames, onFileNameChange,\n}: Props) {\n  const [panelWidth, setPanelWidth] = useState(DEFAULT_WIDTH)\n  const [hoveredGap, setHoveredGap] = useState<number | null>(null)\n\n  const thumbWidth = panelWidth - ITEM_PADDING * 2\n  const thumbHeight = Math.round(thumbWidth * PAGE_ASPECT)\n  const pageItemHeight = thumbHeight + LABEL_HEIGHT + ITEM_PADDING + GAP_HEIGHT\n\n  const items = useMemo(() => buildItems(pageCount, splitPoints), [pageCount, splitPoints])\n  const itemsRef = useRef(items)\n  itemsRef.current = items\n\n  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)\n\n  const virtualizer = useVirtualizer({\n    count: items.length,\n    getScrollElement: () => scrollRef.current,\n    estimateSize: (index) => items[index]?.type === 'header' ? HEADER_HEIGHT : pageItemHeight,\n    overscan: 3,\n  })\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    virtualizer.measure()\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, [items.length, pageItemHeight])\n\n  const virtualItems = virtualizer.getVirtualItems()\n  pageCache.usePageCacheRender()\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    for (const vItem of virtualItems) {\n      const item = items[vItem.index]\n      if (item?.type === 'page') pageCache.load(pdfPath, item.page, thumbWidth)\n    }\n  })\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const index = itemsRef.current.findIndex(item => item.type === 'page' && item.page === selectedPage)\n    if (index >= 0) virtualizer.scrollToIndex(index, { align: 'auto' })\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, [selectedPage])\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement || e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) return\n      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {\n        e.preventDefault()\n        if (selectedPage > 1) onSelectPage(selectedPage - 1)\n      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {\n        e.preventDefault()\n        if (selectedPage < pageCount) onSelectPage(selectedPage + 1)\n      }\n    }\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handler)\n    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handler)\n  }, [selectedPage, pageCount, onSelectPage])\n\n  const startDrag = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {\n    const startX = e.clientX\n    const startWidth = panelWidth\n    const clamp = (w: number) => Math.max(MIN_WIDTH, Math.min(MAX_WIDTH, w))\n    const onMove = (ev: MouseEvent) => setPanelWidth(clamp(startWidth + ev.clientX - startX))\n    const onUp = (ev: MouseEvent) => {\n      setPanelWidth(clamp(startWidth + ev.clientX - startX))\n      document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove)\n      document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp)\n    }\n    document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove)\n    document.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp)\n    e.preventDefault()\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div style={{ display: 'flex', height: '100%', flexShrink: 0 }}>\n      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', width: panelWidth, height: '100%' }}>\n        <div\n          ref={scrollRef}\n          style={{\n            flex: 1,\n            minHeight: 0,\n            overflowY: 'auto',\n            overflowX: 'hidden',\n            background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',\n          }}\n        >\n          <div style={{ height: virtualizer.getTotalSize(), position: 'relative' }}>\n            {virtualItems.map(vItem => {\n              const item = items[vItem.index]\n\n              if (item.type === 'header') {\n                return (\n                  <div\n                    key={vItem.key}\n                    style={{ position: 'absolute', top: vItem.start, left: 0, width: '100%', height: vItem.size }}\n                  >\n                    <OutputFileHeader\n                      filename={fileNames.get(item.firstPage) ?? ''}\n                      onChange={(name) => onFileNameChange(item.firstPage, name)}\n                    />\n                  </div>\n                )\n              }\n\n              const page = item.page\n              const src = pageCache.getSrc(pdfPath, page)\n              const isSelected = page === selectedPage\n              const isSplit = splitPoints.has(page)\n              const isLastPage = page === pageCount\n\n              return (\n                <div\n                  key={vItem.key}\n                  style={{\n                    position: 'absolute',\n                    top: vItem.start,\n                    left: 0,\n                    width: '100%',\n                    height: vItem.size,\n                    boxSizing: 'border-box',\n                  }}\n                >\n                  {/* Thumbnail */}\n                  <div\n                    style={{ padding: ITEM_PADDING, paddingBottom: 0, cursor: 'pointer' }}\n                    onClick={() => onSelectPage(page)}\n                  >\n                    <div\n                      style={{\n                        border: `2px solid ${isSelected ? 'var(--mantine-color-blue-5)' : 'transparent'}`,\n                        borderRadius: 4,\n                        overflow: 'hidden',\n                        background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-1)',\n                      }}\n                    >\n                      {src ? (\n                        <img\n                          src={src}\n                          alt={`Page ${page}`}\n                          style={{ width: '100%', display: 'block' }}\n                          draggable={false}\n                        />\n                      ) : (\n                        <div style={{ width: '100%', height: thumbHeight, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>\n                          {pageCache.isLoading(pdfPath, page) && <Loader size=\"xs\" />}\n                        </div>\n                      )}\n                    </div>\n                    <div\n                      style={{\n                        textAlign: 'center',\n                        fontSize: 11,\n                        color: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-7)',\n                        height: LABEL_HEIGHT,\n                        lineHeight: `${LABEL_HEIGHT}px`,\n                      }}\n                    >\n                      {page}\n                    </div>\n                  </div>\n\n                  {/* Gap zone between thumbnails */}\n                  {!isLastPage && (\n                    <GapZone\n                      isSplit={isSplit}\n                      isHovered={hoveredGap === page}\n                      onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onToggleSplitPoint(page) }}\n                      onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredGap(page)}\n                      onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredGap(null)}\n                    />\n                  )}\n                </div>\n              )\n            })}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div\n        onMouseDown={startDrag}\n        style={{\n          width: DRAG_HANDLE_WIDTH,\n          height: '100%',\n          cursor: 'col-resize',\n          flexShrink: 0,\n          background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',\n        }}\n      />\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n\nfunction OutputFileHeader({ filename, onChange }: { filename: string; onChange: (name: string) => void }) {\n  return (\n    <div\n      style={{\n        margin: `4px ${ITEM_PADDING}px`,\n        height: HEADER_HEIGHT - 8,\n        padding: '0 6px',\n        background: 'var(--mantine-color-white)',\n        border: '1px solid var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',\n        borderRadius: 4,\n        display: 'flex',\n        alignItems: 'center',\n        gap: 2,\n      }}\n    >\n      <input\n        type=\"text\"\n        value={filename}\n        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}\n        placeholder=\"filename\"\n        style={{\n          flex: 1,\n          border: 'none',\n          outline: 'none',\n          background: 'transparent',\n          fontSize: 12,\n          fontWeight: 500,\n          color: 'inherit',\n          minWidth: 0,\n        }}\n      />\n      <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--mantine-color-dimmed)', flexShrink: 0 }}>.pdf</span>\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n\ninterface GapZoneProps {\n  isSplit: boolean\n  isHovered: boolean\n  onClick: (e: React.MouseEvent) => void\n  onMouseEnter: () => void\n  onMouseLeave: () => void\n}\n\nfunction GapZone({ isSplit, isHovered, onClick, onMouseEnter, onMouseLeave }: GapZoneProps) {\n  const bg = isSplit\n    ? 'var(--mantine-color-blue-0)'\n    : isHovered\n    ? 'var(--mantine-color-gray-2)'\n    : undefined\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      onClick={onClick}\n      onMouseEnter={onMouseEnter}\n      onMouseLeave={onMouseLeave}\n      style={{\n        height: GAP_HEIGHT,\n        background: bg,\n        display: 'flex',\n        alignItems: 'center',\n        paddingLeft: ITEM_PADDING,\n        paddingRight: ITEM_PADDING,\n        cursor: 'pointer',\n      }}\n    >\n      {isSplit && (\n        <div style={{ flex: 1, height: 2, background: 'var(--mantine-color-blue-5)', borderRadius: 1 }} />\n      )}\n      {!isSplit && isHovered && (\n        <div style={{ flex: 1, height: 0, borderTop: '1px dashed var(--mantine-color-gray-5)' }} />\n      )}\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ben/git/paper-scan-processor/frontend/src/components/SplitMode/index.tsx",
  "content": "import { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react'\nimport { Box, Button, Group } from '@mantine/core'\nimport { notifications } from '@mantine/notifications'\nimport ThumbnailPanel from './ThumbnailPanel'\nimport DetailPanel from '../DetailPanel'\nimport { OpenPDF, PageCount, PickFolder, ExportSplit } from '../../../wailsjs/go/main/App'\n\ninterface Props {\n  initialPath?: string | null\n}\n\nexport default function SplitMode({ initialPath }: Props) {\n  const [pdfPath, setPdfPath] = useState<string | null>(null)\n  const [pageCount, setPageCount] = useState(0)\n  const [selectedPage, setSelectedPage] = useState(1)\n  const [splitPoints, setSplitPoints] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set())\n  const [fileNames, setFileNames] = useState<Map<number, string>>(new Map([[1, '']]))\n  const [exporting, setExporting] = useState(false)\n\n  const splitPointsRef = useRef(splitPoints)\n  splitPointsRef.current = splitPoints\n\n  const resetForFile = (count: number, path: string) => {\n    setPdfPath(path)\n    setPageCount(count)\n    setSelectedPage(1)\n    setSplitPoints(new Set())\n    setFileNames(new Map([[1, '']]))\n  }\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!initialPath) return\n    PageCount(initialPath)\n      .then(count => resetForFile(count, initialPath))\n      .catch(e => notifications.show({ title: 'Failed to open file', message: String(e), color: 'red' }))\n  }, [initialPath])\n\n  const handleOpen = async () => {\n    const path = await OpenPDF()\n    if (!path) return\n    try {\n      const count = await PageCount(path)\n      resetForFile(count, path)\n    } catch (e) {\n      notifications.show({ title: 'Failed to open file', message: String(e), color: 'red' })\n    }\n  }\n\n  const handleExport = async () => {\n    if (!pdfPath) return\n    const outDir = await PickFolder()\n    if (!outDir) return\n    setExporting(true)\n    try {\n      const sorted = [...splitPoints].sort((a, b) => a - b)\n      const firstPages = [1, ...sorted.map(p => p + 1)]\n      const outNames = firstPages.map(fp => fileNames.get(fp) ?? '')\n      await ExportSplit(pdfPath, sorted, outDir, outNames)\n      const fileCount = splitPoints.size + 1\n      notifications.show({\n        title: 'Export complete',\n        message: `${fileCount} file${fileCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''} saved to ${outDir}`,\n        color: 'green',\n      })\n    } catch (e) {\n      notifications.show({ title: 'Export failed', message: String(e), color: 'red' })\n    } finally {\n      setExporting(false)\n    }\n  }\n\n  const toggleSplitPoint = useCallback((afterPage: number) => {\n    const isAdding = !splitPointsRef.current.has(afterPage)\n    setSplitPoints(prev => {\n      const next = new Set(prev)\n      if (next.has(afterPage)) { next.delete(afterPage) } else { next.add(afterPage) }\n      return next\n    })\n    setFileNames(prev => {\n      const next = new Map(prev)\n      if (isAdding) { next.set(afterPage + 1, '') } else { next.delete(afterPage + 1) }\n      return next\n    })\n  }, [])\n\n  const handleFileNameChange = useCallback((firstPage: number, name: string) => {\n    setFileNames(prev => new Map(prev).set(firstPage, name))\n  }, [])\n\n  return (\n    <Box style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100%' }}>\n      <Box\n        style={{\n          flexShrink: 0,\n          borderBottom: '1px solid var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',\n          display: 'flex',\n          alignItems: 'center',\n          paddingInline: 12,\n          height: 44,\n        }}\n      >\n        <Group gap={8} style={{ width: '100%', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>\n          <Button size=\"xs\" variant=\"default\" onClick={handleOpen}>\n            Open PDF\n          </Button>\n          <Button size=\"xs\" disabled={!pdfPath} loading={exporting} onClick={handleExport}>\n            Export\n          </Button>\n        </Group>\n      </Box>\n\n      <Box style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'hidden', display: 'flex' }}>\n        {pdfPath ? (\n          <>\n            <ThumbnailPanel\n              pdfPath={pdfPath}\n              pageCount={pageCount}\n              selectedPage={selectedPage}\n              onSelectPage={setSelectedPage}\n              splitPoints={splitPoints}\n              onToggleSplitPoint={toggleSplitPoint}\n              fileNames={fileNames}\n              onFileNameChange={handleFileNameChange}\n            />\n            <DetailPanel\n              pdfPath={pdfPath}\n              pageNum={selectedPage}\n              pageCount={pageCount}\n              onNavigate={setSelectedPage}\n            />\n          </>\n        ) : (\n          <Box style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>\n            <Button onClick={handleOpen}>Open PDF</Button>\n          </Box>\n        )}\n      </Box>\n    </Box>\n  )\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Done. Two commits:

- `0fa5e6a` — mixed header/page virtual list with filename inputs; `ExportSplit` now takes `outNames`
- `b5e74f3` — spec checked off

Each output file group now has a white card with an editable filename field and a `.pdf` suffix at the top. The card for file 1 is always present; cards for subsequent files appear/disappear as split points are toggled. On export, blank names fall back to `output-N`.

> DEVELOPER

what's the reason to store `items` in a ref?

> AGENT

To avoid a stale closure without adding `items` to the `useEffect` dependency array.

The scroll effect only depends on `[selectedPage]` — we don't want it to re-fire just because a split point was toggled. But without the ref, the closure captures the `items` value from the render when `selectedPage` last changed, which could be outdated if split points were added/removed since then. That would cause `findIndex` to search the wrong list and scroll to the wrong position.

The ref gives us the current `items` at fire time without making `items` a dependency.

That said, including `items` in the deps would also be fine in practice — the only downside is an extra (harmless) re-scroll to the current selected page whenever split points change. The ref is a minor optimisation I wouldn't bother with unless there was a reason to avoid that extra scroll. Want me to simplify it?

108	1. The user loads an input PDF via a file picker dialog or by dragging and dropping a file onto the app window.
109	2. The app displays all pages as thumbnails in the left panel. Clicking a thumbnail selects it and updates the detail panel on the right.
110	3. The user defines split points by clicking in the gaps between page thumbnails. A visual divider appears at each split point; clicking again removes it. Dividers can also be repositioned by dragging them to a different gap. Each divider marks where a new output file begins.
111	4. The user sets a global filename template using `{date}` (today's date as `YYYY-MM-DD`) and `{name}` (a per-file label). Example: `{date} {name}` → `2026-06-12 invoice.pdf`. The app prefills the filename for each output file using this template. The user can then edit any individual file's name before exporting.
112	5. The user sets a global output folder (an existing folder on the local filesystem, or a Google Drive folder if Drive integration is enabled). For each output file, the user can adjust:
113	   - The prefilled filename (editable)
114	   - The destination folder (overridable per file)
115	6. The user clicks Export. Before splitting, the app checks for filename conflicts at each destination. If any conflict is found, the export is aborted and an error message identifies the conflicting files. Once resolved, the app splits the input PDF and writes (or uploads) each output file. Afterwards, the app prompts the user to keep, move, or delete the input file.
116	
117	### Layout

8	interface Props {
9	  initialPath?: string | null
10	}

12	export default function SplitMode({ initialPath }: Props) {
13	  const [pdfPath, setPdfPath] = useState<string | null>(null)
14	  const [pageCount, setPageCount] = useState(0)
15	  const [selectedPage, setSelectedPage] = useState(1)
16	  const [splitPoints, setSplitPoints] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set())
17	  const [fileNames, setFileNames] = useState<Map<number, string>>(new Map([[1, '']]))
18	  const [exporting, setExporting] = useState(false)
19	
20	  const splitPointsRef = useRef(splitPoints)
21	  splitPointsRef.current = splitPoints
22	
23	  const resetForFile = (count: number, path: string) => {
24	    setPdfPath(path)
25	    setPageCount(count)
26	    setSelectedPage(1)
27	    setSplitPoints(new Set())
28	    setFileNames(new Map([[1, '']]))
29	  }
30	
31	  useEffect(() => {
32	    if (!initialPath) return
33	    PageCount(initialPath)
34	      .then(count => resetForFile(count, initialPath))
35	      .catch(e => notifications.show({ title: 'Failed to open file', message: String(e), color: 'red' }))
36	  }, [initialPath])
37	
38	  const handleOpen = async () => {
39	    const path = await OpenPDF()
40	    if (!path) return
41	    try {
42	      const count = await PageCount(path)
43	      resetForFile(count, path)
44	    } catch (e) {
45	      notifications.show({ title: 'Failed to open file', message: String(e), color: 'red' })
46	    }
47	  }
48	
49	  const handleExport = async () => {
50	    if (!pdfPath) return
51	    const outDir = await PickFolder()
52	    if (!outDir) return
53	    setExporting(true)
54	    try {
55	      const sorted = [...splitPoints].sort((a, b) => a - b)
56	      const firstPages = [1, ...sorted.map(p => p + 1)]
57	      const outNames = firstPages.map(fp => fileNames.get(fp) ?? '')
58	      await ExportSplit(pdfPath, sorted, outDir, outNames)
59	      const fileCount = splitPoints.size + 1
60	      notifications.show({
61	        title: 'Export complete',
62	        message: `${fileCount} file${fileCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''} saved to ${outDir}`,
63	        color: 'green',
64	      })
65	    } catch (e) {
66	      notifications.show({ title: 'Export failed', message: String(e), color: 'red' })
67	    } finally {
68	      setExporting(false)
69	    }
70	  }
71	
72	  const toggleSplitPoint = useCallback((afterPage: number) => {
73	    const isAdding = !splitPointsRef.current.has(afterPage)
74	    setSplitPoints(prev => {
75	      const next = new Set(prev)
76	      if (next.has(afterPage)) { next.delete(afterPage) } else { next.add(afterPage) }
77	      return next
78	    })
79	    setFileNames(prev => {
80	      const next = new Map(prev)
81	      if (isAdding) { next.set(afterPage + 1, '') } else { next.delete(afterPage + 1) }
82	      return next
83	    })
84	  }, [])
85	
86	  const handleFileNameChange = useCallback((firstPage: number, name: string) => {
87	    setFileNames(prev => new Map(prev).set(firstPage, name))
88	  }, [])
89	
90	  return (
91	    <Box style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100%' }}>
92	      <Box
93	        style={{
94	          flexShrink: 0,
95	          borderBottom: '1px solid var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',
96	          display: 'flex',
97	          alignItems: 'center',
98	          paddingInline: 12,
99	          height: 44,
100	        }}
101	      >
102	        <Group gap={8} style={{ width: '100%', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
103	          <Button size="xs" variant="default" onClick={handleOpen}>
104	            Open PDF
105	          </Button>
106	          <Button size="xs" disabled={!pdfPath} loading={exporting} onClick={handleExport}>
107	            Export
108	          </Button>
109	        </Group>
110	      </Box>
111	
112	      <Box style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'hidden', display: 'flex' }}>
113	        {pdfPath ? (
114	          <>
115	            <ThumbnailPanel
116	              pdfPath={pdfPath}
117	              pageCount={pageCount}
118	              selectedPage={selectedPage}
119	              onSelectPage={setSelectedPage}
120	              splitPoints={splitPoints}
121	              onToggleSplitPoint={toggleSplitPoint}
122	              fileNames={fileNames}
123	              onFileNameChange={handleFileNameChange}
124	            />
125	            <DetailPanel
126	              pdfPath={pdfPath}
127	              pageNum={selectedPage}
128	              pageCount={pageCount}
129	              onNavigate={setSelectedPage}
130	            />
131	          </>
132	        ) : (
133	          <Box style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
134	            <Button onClick={handleOpen}>Open PDF</Button>
135	          </Box>
136	        )}
137	      </Box>
138	    </Box>
139	  )
140	}

> DEVELOPER

Is it necessary to store the template in a ref?

> AGENT

Not strictly. `templateRef` serves two purposes here:

1. **In `toggleSplitPoint`** — keeps the callback stable (no `template` dep) so it doesn't recreate on every keystroke in the template input. But since `ThumbnailPanel` isn't wrapped in `React.memo`, that stability buys nothing.

2. **In the `initialPath` effect** — reads the latest template even if the user changed it while `PageCount` was resolving asynchronously. That's a real-but-tiny edge case.

> DEVELOPER

The `{name}` template variable not be written into the generated filename. Instead the cursor should be placed at the posittion where `{name}` was.

> AGENT

Good catch. So `{name}` should be removed from the prefilled string and the cursor placed at that position when the user first focuses the input. Let me think through the implementation before touching anything.

`applyTemplate` needs to return both the value (with `{name}` stripped) and the cursor offset. That cursor position needs to reach `OutputFileHeader` so it can be applied on the first `onFocus`. To avoid spurious repositioning if the header scrolls out of view and back in, the parent needs to clear the pending cursor once it's been applied.

Plan:
- `applyTemplate` → `{ value: string, cursorPos: number }`
- Add `pendingCursorPos: Map<number, number>` state in `SplitMode`; populated on file open and split-point add; cleared via `onCursorPositioned(firstPage)` callback
- Thread `pendingCursorPos` + `onCursorPositioned` through `ThumbnailPanel` → `OutputFileHeader`
- `OutputFileHeader` positions cursor on first `onFocus` and calls `onCursorPositioned`

No cleanups needed first. Let me implement:

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ben/git/paper-scan-processor/frontend/src/components/SplitMode/index.tsx",
  "old_string": "function applyTemplate(template: string): string {\n  const date = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]\n  return template.replace('{date}', date)\n  // {name} is left as-is for the user to fill in per file\n}",
  "new_string": "function applyTemplate(template: string): { value: string; cursorPos: number } {\n  const date = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]\n  const withDate = template.replace('{date}', date)\n  const nameIdx = withDate.indexOf('{name}')\n  if (nameIdx === -1) return { value: withDate, cursorPos: withDate.length }\n  return { value: withDate.replace('{name}', ''), cursorPos: nameIdx }\n}"
}

  "new_string": "  const toggleSplitPoint = useCallback((afterPage: number) => {\n    const isAdding = !splitPointsRef.current.has(afterPage)\n    const prefill = isAdding ? applyTemplate(template) : null\n    setSplitPoints(prev => {\n      const next = new Set(prev)\n      if (next.has(afterPage)) { next.delete(afterPage) } else { next.add(afterPage) }\n      return next\n    })\n    setFileNames(prev => {\n      const next = new Map(prev)\n      if (prefill) { next.set(afterPage + 1, prefill.value) } else { next.delete(afterPage + 1) }\n      return next\n    })\n    setPendingCursorPos(prev => {\n      const next = new Map(prev)\n      if (prefill) { next.set(afterPage + 1, prefill.cursorPos) } else { next.delete(afterPage + 1) }\n      return next\n    })\n  }, [template])"

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ben/git/paper-scan-processor/frontend/src/components/SplitMode/ThumbnailPanel.tsx",
  "old_string": "function OutputFileHeader({ filename, onChange }: { filename: string; onChange: (name: string) => void }) {\n  return (\n    <div\n      style={{\n        margin: `4px ${ITEM_PADDING}px`,\n        height: HEADER_HEIGHT - 8,\n        padding: '0 6px',\n        background: 'var(--mantine-color-white)',\n        border: '1px solid var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',\n        borderRadius: 4,\n        display: 'flex',\n        alignItems: 'center',\n        gap: 2,\n      }}\n    >\n      <input\n        type=\"text\"\n        value={filename}\n        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}\n        placeholder=\"filename\"\n        style={{\n          flex: 1,\n          border: 'none',\n          outline: 'none',\n          background: 'transparent',\n          fontSize: 12,\n          fontWeight: 500,\n          color: 'inherit',\n          minWidth: 0,\n        }}\n      />\n      <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--mantine-color-dimmed)', flexShrink: 0 }}>.pdf</span>\n    </div>\n  )\n}",
  "new_string": "function OutputFileHeader({\n  filename, onChange, cursorPos, onCursorPositioned,\n}: {\n  filename: string\n  onChange: (name: string) => void\n  cursorPos: number | null\n  onCursorPositioned: () => void\n}) {\n  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)\n\n  const handleFocus = () => {\n    if (cursorPos !== null && inputRef.current) {\n      inputRef.current.setSelectionRange(cursorPos, cursorPos)\n      onCursorPositioned()\n    }\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      style={{\n        margin: `4px ${ITEM_PADDING}px`,\n        height: HEADER_HEIGHT - 8,\n        padding: '0 6px',\n        background: 'var(--mantine-color-white)',\n        border: '1px solid var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',\n        borderRadius: 4,\n        display: 'flex',\n        alignItems: 'center',\n        gap: 2,\n      }}\n    >\n      <input\n        ref={inputRef}\n        type=\"text\"\n        value={filename}\n        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}\n        onFocus={handleFocus}\n        placeholder=\"filename\"\n        style={{\n          flex: 1,\n          border: 'none',\n          outline: 'none',\n          background: 'transparent',\n          fontSize: 12,\n          fontWeight: 500,\n          color: 'inherit',\n          minWidth: 0,\n        }}\n      />\n      <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--mantine-color-dimmed)', flexShrink: 0 }}>.pdf</span>\n    </div>\n  )\n}"
}

> DEVELOPER

Why not autofocus the input element after the divider was added? Then the cursor can immediately be positioned and the position doesn't need to be persisted.

> AGENT

Much simpler — no persistent state needed at all. When a split point is added, auto-focus the new header's input via a callback ref, position the cursor immediately, and done. `pendingCursorPos` goes away entirely; just a single `pendingFocus: { firstPage, cursorPos } | null` prop.

10	function applyTemplate(template: string): { value: string; cursorPos: number } {
11	  const date = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]
12	  const withDate = template.replace('{date}', date)
13	  const nameIdx = withDate.indexOf('{name}')
14	  if (nameIdx === -1) return { value: withDate, cursorPos: withDate.length }
15	  return { value: withDate.replace('{name}', ''), cursorPos: nameIdx }
16	}

18	interface Props {
19	  initialPath?: string | null
20	}

22	export default function SplitMode({ initialPath }: Props) {
23	  const [pdfPath, setPdfPath] = useState<string | null>(null)
24	  const [pageCount, setPageCount] = useState(0)
25	  const [selectedPage, setSelectedPage] = useState(1)
26	  const [splitPoints, setSplitPoints] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set())
27	  const [fileNames, setFileNames] = useState<Map<number, string>>(new Map([[1, '']]))
28	  const [pendingCursorPos, setPendingCursorPos] = useState<Map<number, number>>(new Map())
29	  const [template, setTemplate] = useState(DEFAULT_TEMPLATE)
30	  const [exporting, setExporting] = useState(false)
31	
32	  const splitPointsRef = useRef(splitPoints)
33	  splitPointsRef.current = splitPoints
34	
35	  const resetForFile = (count: number, path: string, tmpl: string) => {
36	    const { value, cursorPos } = applyTemplate(tmpl)
37	    setPdfPath(path)
38	    setPageCount(count)
39	    setSelectedPage(1)
40	    setSplitPoints(new Set())
41	    setFileNames(new Map([[1, value]]))
42	    setPendingCursorPos(new Map([[1, cursorPos]]))
43	  }
44	
45	  useEffect(() => {
46	    if (!initialPath) return
47	    PageCount(initialPath)
48	      .then(count => resetForFile(count, initialPath, template))
49	      .catch(e => notifications.show({ title: 'Failed to open file', message: String(e), color: 'red' }))
50	  }, [initialPath])
51	
52	  const handleOpen = async () => {
53	    const path = await OpenPDF()
54	    if (!path) return
55	    try {
56	      const count = await PageCount(path)
57	      resetForFile(count, path, template)
58	    } catch (e) {
59	      notifications.show({ title: 'Failed to open file', message: String(e), color: 'red' })
60	    }
61	  }
62	
63	  const handleExport = async () => {
64	    if (!pdfPath) return
65	    const outDir = await PickFolder()
66	    if (!outDir) return
67	    setExporting(true)
68	    try {
69	      const sorted = [...splitPoints].sort((a, b) => a - b)
70	      const firstPages = [1, ...sorted.map(p => p + 1)]
71	      const outNames = firstPages.map(fp => fileNames.get(fp) ?? '')
72	      await ExportSplit(pdfPath, sorted, outDir, outNames)
73	      const fileCount = splitPoints.size + 1
74	      notifications.show({
75	        title: 'Export complete',
76	        message: `${fileCount} file${fileCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''} saved to ${outDir}`,
77	        color: 'green',
78	      })
79	    } catch (e) {
80	      notifications.show({ title: 'Export failed', message: String(e), color: 'red' })
81	    } finally {
82	      setExporting(false)
83	    }
84	  }
85	
86	  const toggleSplitPoint = useCallback((afterPage: number) => {
87	    const isAdding = !splitPointsRef.current.has(afterPage)
88	    const prefill = isAdding ? applyTemplate(template) : null
89	    setSplitPoints(prev => {
90	      const next = new Set(prev)
91	      if (next.has(afterPage)) { next.delete(afterPage) } else { next.add(afterPage) }
92	      return next
93	    })
94	    setFileNames(prev => {
95	      const next = new Map(prev)
96	      if (prefill) { next.set(afterPage + 1, prefill.value) } else { next.delete(afterPage + 1) }
97	      return next
98	    })
99	    setPendingCursorPos(prev => {
100	      const next = new Map(prev)
101	      if (prefill) { next.set(afterPage + 1, prefill.cursorPos) } else { next.delete(afterPage + 1) }
102	      return next
103	    })
104	  }, [template])
105	
106	  const handleFileNameChange = useCallback((firstPage: number, name: string) => {
107	    setFileNames(prev => new Map(prev).set(firstPage, name))
108	  }, [])
109	
110	  const handleCursorPositioned = useCallback((firstPage: number) => {
111	    setPendingCursorPos(prev => {
112	      const next = new Map(prev)
113	      next.delete(firstPage)
114	      return next
115	    })
116	  }, [])
117	
118	  return (
119	    <Box style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100%' }}>
120	      <Box
121	        style={{
122	          flexShrink: 0,
123	          borderBottom: '1px solid var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',
124	          display: 'flex',
125	          alignItems: 'center',
126	          paddingInline: 12,
127	          height: 44,
128	        }}
129	      >
130	        <Group gap={8} style={{ width: '100%' }}>
131	          <Button size="xs" variant="default" onClick={handleOpen}>
132	            Open PDF
133	          </Button>
134	          <TextInput
135	            size="xs"
136	            placeholder={DEFAULT_TEMPLATE}
137	            value={template}
138	            onChange={(e) => setTemplate(e.currentTarget.value)}
139	            leftSection={<span style={{ fontSize: 11, whiteSpace: 'nowrap', color: 'var(--mantine-color-dimmed)' }}>Template</span>}
140	            leftSectionWidth={60}
141	            style={{ flex: 1 }}
142	          />
143	          <Button size="xs" disabled={!pdfPath} loading={exporting} onClick={handleExport}>
144	            Export
145	          </Button>
146	        </Group>
147	      </Box>
148	
149	      <Box style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'hidden', display: 'flex' }}>
150	        {pdfPath ? (
151	          <>
152	            <ThumbnailPanel
153	              pdfPath={pdfPath}
154	              pageCount={pageCount}
155	              selectedPage={selectedPage}
156	              onSelectPage={setSelectedPage}
157	              splitPoints={splitPoints}
158	              onToggleSplitPoint={toggleSplitPoint}
159	              fileNames={fileNames}
160	              onFileNameChange={handleFileNameChange}
161	              pendingCursorPos={pendingCursorPos}
162	              onCursorPositioned={handleCursorPositioned}
163	            />
164	            <DetailPanel
165	              pdfPath={pdfPath}
166	              pageNum={selectedPage}
167	              pageCount={pageCount}
168	              onNavigate={setSelectedPage}
169	            />
170	          </>

173	            <Button onClick={handleOpen}>Open PDF</Button>

16	function buildItems(pageCount: number, splitPoints: Set<number>): ListItem[] {
17	  const result: ListItem[] = []
18	  let fileIndex = 0
19	  for (let page = 1; page <= pageCount; page++) {
20	    if (page === 1 || splitPoints.has(page - 1)) {
21	      result.push({ type: 'header', fileIndex: fileIndex++, firstPage: page })
22	    }
23	    result.push({ type: 'page', page })
24	  }
25	  return result
26	}
27	
28	interface Props {
29	  pdfPath: string
30	  pageCount: number
31	  selectedPage: number
32	  onSelectPage: (page: number) => void
33	  splitPoints: Set<number>
34	  onToggleSplitPoint: (afterPage: number) => void
35	  fileNames: Map<number, string>
36	  onFileNameChange: (firstPage: number, name: string) => void
37	  pendingFocus: { firstPage: number; cursorPos: number } | null
38	  onFocused: () => void
39	}
40	
41	export default function SplitThumbnailPanel({
42	  pdfPath, pageCount, selectedPage, onSelectPage,
43	  splitPoints, onToggleSplitPoint,
44	  fileNames, onFileNameChange,
45	  pendingFocus, onFocused,
46	}: Props) {
47	  const [panelWidth, setPanelWidth] = useState(DEFAULT_WIDTH)
48	  const [hoveredGap, setHoveredGap] = useState<number | null>(null)
49	
50	  const thumbWidth = panelWidth - ITEM_PADDING * 2
```
