```
108	1. The user loads an input PDF via a file picker dialog or by dragging and dropping a file onto the app window.
109	2. The app displays all pages as thumbnails in the left panel. Clicking a thumbnail selects it and updates the detail panel on the right.
110	3. The user defines split points by clicking in the gaps between page thumbnails. A visual divider appears at each split point; clicking again removes it. Dividers can also be repositioned by dragging them to a different gap. Each divider marks where a new output file begins.
111	4. The user sets a global filename template using `{date}` (today's date as `YYYY-MM-DD`) and `{name}` (a per-file label). Example: `{date} {name}` → `2026-06-12 invoice.pdf`. The app prefills the filename for each output file using this template. The user can then edit any individual file's name before exporting.
112	5. The user sets a global output folder (an existing folder on the local filesystem, or a Google Drive folder if Drive integration is enabled). For each output file, the user can adjust:
113	   - The prefilled filename (editable)
114	   - The destination folder (overridable per file)
115	6. The user clicks Export. Before splitting, the app checks for filename conflicts at each destination. If any conflict is found, the export is aborted and an error message identifies the conflicting files. Once resolved, the app splits the input PDF and writes (or uploads) each output file. Afterwards, the app prompts the user to keep, move, or delete the input file.
116	
117	### Layout
118	
119	Split mode uses a two-column layout:
120	
121	- **Left panel** (adjustable width, drag handle on right edge) — a single vertically scrolling area that combines the thumbnail strip and output file controls.
122	- **Right panel** (fills remaining space) — the page detail view.
123	
124	#### Left panel structure
125	
126	The left panel is a continuous scroll area. Pages are grouped by output file. Each group is preceded by a compact **output file header** containing the editable filename field and (when applicable) destination folder. Split-point dividers between groups are the visual boundary between one output file and the next.
127	
128	```
129	┌─ invoice.pdf ──────────────────────┐
130	│ folder: /Documents                 │
131	└────────────────────────────────────┘
132	  [page 1 thumbnail]
133	  [page 2 thumbnail]
134	  [page 3 thumbnail]
135	  ──────── [gap / split point] ───────
136	┌─ receipt.pdf ──────────────────────┐
137	│ folder: /Documents                 │
138	└────────────────────────────────────┘
139	  [page 4 thumbnail]
140	  [page 5 thumbnail]
141	```
142	
143	Clicking a gap toggles a split point there and creates a new output file section. The filename and folder fields for each section appear immediately above its pages.
144	
145	### Keyboard shortcuts
146	
147	| Key     | Action                                                  |
148	| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
149	| `Space` | Toggle a split point after the selected page            |
150	| `Tab`   | Move focus to the next filename input in the left panel |
151	
152	### Google Drive integration (optional)
153	
154	- Authentication uses Google OAuth via a browser window, triggered the first time Drive is enabled. Credentials are stored locally and reused in future sessions.
155	- When Drive is enabled, each output file header in the left panel shows a Drive folder field. Clicking it opens a folder browser modal that displays the user's Drive folder tree. The user navigates the tree and selects a destination folder. The selected path is shown in the header (e.g. `My Drive / Clients / Smith / 2026`).
156	- The folder tree is fetched lazily — the first time the user opens the folder browser in a session.
157	- The folder browser shows a **recently used folders** list at the top for quick access. This list persists across sessions.
158	- The global output folder setting can be set to a Drive folder, which prefills all output file headers. Individual headers can be overridden.
159	- After export, each output file is uploaded to its designated Drive folder. If an upload fails, the affected header shows an inline error message and a Retry button; other uploads are unaffected.
160	- When Drive is enabled, the local subfolder name for each output file is derived automatically from the Drive folder path (see Local folder sorting below).
161	
162	### Local folder sorting
```

The key design choices for step 5:

1. **Toolbar**: add a folder-picker button (shows basename of selected folder) between the template and Export
2. **Each output file header**: add a second row showing the effective folder (override or global), clicking picks an override
3. **Backend**: `ExportSplit` needs `outDirs []string` instead of a single `outDir string` since each file can go to a different folder
4. **Export**: disable if no global folder is set; build per-file `outDirs` array using override ?? global

An empty name falls back to \"output-N\". splitAfter contains\n// 1-indexed page numbers after which a new file begins.\nfunc (a *App) ExportSplit(inPath string, splitAfter []int, outDir string, outNames []string) error {\n\ttmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp(\"\", \"psp-split-*\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tdefer os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)\n\n\tpaths, err := splitPDF(inPath, splitAfter, tmpDir)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tfor i, src := range paths {\n\t\tname := \"\"\n\t\tif i < len(outNames) {\n\t\t\tname = strings.TrimSpace(outNames[i])\n\t\t}\n\t\tif name == \"\" {\n\t\t\tname = fmt.Sprintf(\"output-%d\", i+1)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !strings.HasSuffix(strings.ToLower(name), \".pdf\") {\n\t\t\tname += \".pdf\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := copyFile(src, filepath.Join(outDir, name)); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}",
  "new_string": "// ExportSplit splits the PDF at inPath at the given page boundaries and writes\n// each segment to the corresponding directory in outDirs using the corresponding\n// name from outNames (without extension). An empty name falls back to \"output-N\".\n// splitAfter contains 1-indexed page numbers after which a new file begins.\nfunc (a *App) ExportSplit(inPath string, splitAfter []int, outDirs []string, outNames []string) error {\n\ttmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp(\"\", \"psp-split-*\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tdefer os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)\n\n\tpaths, err := splitPDF(inPath, splitAfter, tmpDir)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tfor i, src := range paths {\n\t\toutDir := \"\"\n\t\tif i < len(outDirs) {\n\t\t\toutDir = outDirs[i]\n\t\t}\n\t\tname := \"\"\n\t\tif i < len(outNames) {\n\t\t\tname = strings.TrimSpace(outNames[i])\n\t\t}\n\t\tif name == \"\" {\n\t\t\tname = fmt.Sprintf(\"output-%d\", i+1)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !strings.HasSuffix(strings.ToLower(name), \".pdf\") {\n\t\t\tname += \".pdf\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := copyFile(src, filepath.Join(outDir, name)); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}"
}

1	import { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react'
2	import { Box, Button, Group, TextInput } from '@mantine/core'
3	import { notifications } from '@mantine/notifications'
4	import ThumbnailPanel from './ThumbnailPanel'
5	import DetailPanel from '../DetailPanel'
6	import { OpenPDF, PageCount, PickFolder, ExportSplit } from '../../../wailsjs/go/main/App'
7	
8	const DEFAULT_TEMPLATE = '{date} {name}'
9	
10	function applyTemplate(template: string): { value: string; cursorPos: number } {
11	  const date = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]
12	  const withDate = template.replace('{date}', date)
13	  const nameIdx = withDate.indexOf('{name}')
14	  if (nameIdx === -1) return { value: withDate, cursorPos: withDate.length }
15	  return { value: withDate.replace('{name}', ''), cursorPos: nameIdx }
16	}
17	
18	// Tracks which filename input should steal focus after a split point is added,
19	// and where the cursor should land within it. Cleared by the input itself once
20	// it has focused, so only the first render after the split point is added fires.
21	function usePendingFocus() {
22	  const [pendingFocus, setPendingFocus] = useState<{ afterPage: number; cursorPos: number } | null>(null)
23	  const request = useCallback((afterPage: number, cursorPos: number) => setPendingFocus({ afterPage, cursorPos }), [])
24	  const clear = useCallback(() => setPendingFocus(null), [])
25	  return { pendingFocus, request, clear }
26	}
27	
28	interface Props {
29	  initialPath?: string | null
30	}
31	
32	export default function SplitMode({ initialPath }: Props) {
33	  const [pdfPath, setPdfPath] = useState<string | null>(null)
34	  const [pageCount, setPageCount] = useState(0)
35	  const [selectedPage, setSelectedPage] = useState(1)
36	  const [splitPoints, setSplitPoints] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set())
37	  const [fileNames, setFileNames] = useState<Map<number, string>>(new Map([[1, '']]))
38	  const focus = usePendingFocus()
39	  const [template, setTemplate] = useState(DEFAULT_TEMPLATE)
40	  const [exporting, setExporting] = useState(false)
41	
42	  const splitPointsRef = useRef(splitPoints)
43	  splitPointsRef.current = splitPoints
44	
45	  const resetForFile = (count: number, path: string, tmpl: string) => {
46	    setPdfPath(path)
47	    setPageCount(count)
48	    setSelectedPage(1)
49	    setSplitPoints(new Set())
50	    setFileNames(new Map([[1, applyTemplate(tmpl).value]]))
51	    focus.clear()
52	  }
53	
54	  useEffect(() => {
55	    if (!initialPath) return
56	    PageCount(initialPath)
57	      .then(count => resetForFile(count, initialPath, template))
58	      .catch(e => notifications.show({ title: 'Failed to open file', message: String(e), color: 'red' }))
59	  }, [initialPath])
60	
61	  const handleOpen = async () => {
62	    const path = await OpenPDF()
63	    if (!path) return
64	    try {
65	      const count = await PageCount(path)
66	      resetForFile(count, path, template)
67	    } catch (e) {
68	      notifications.show({ title: 'Failed to open file', message: String(e), color: 'red' })
69	    }
70	  }
71	
72	  const handleExport = async () => {
73	    if (!pdfPath) return
74	    const outDir = await PickFolder()
75	    if (!outDir) return
76	    setExporting(true)
77	    try {
78	      const sorted = [...splitPoints].sort((a, b) => a - b)
79	      const firstPages = [1, ...sorted.map(p => p + 1)]
80	      const outNames = firstPages.map(fp => fileNames.get(fp) ?? '')
81	      await ExportSplit(pdfPath, sorted, outDir, outNames)
82	      const fileCount = splitPoints.size + 1
83	      notifications.show({
84	        title: 'Export complete',
85	        message: `${fileCount} file${fileCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''} saved to ${outDir}`,
86	        color: 'green',
87	      })
88	    } catch (e) {
89	      notifications.show({ title: 'Export failed', message: String(e), color: 'red' })
90	    } finally {
91	      setExporting(false)
92	    }
93	  }
94	
95	  const toggleSplitPoint = useCallback((afterPage: number) => {
96	    const isAdding = !splitPointsRef.current.has(afterPage)
97	    const prefill = isAdding ? applyTemplate(template) : null
98	    setSplitPoints(prev => {
99	      const next = new Set(prev)
100	      if (next.has(afterPage)) { next.delete(afterPage) } else { next.add(afterPage) }
101	      return next
102	    })
103	    setFileNames(prev => {
104	      const next = new Map(prev)
105	      if (prefill) { next.set(afterPage + 1, prefill.value) } else { next.delete(afterPage + 1) }
106	      return next
107	    })
108	    if (prefill) focus.request(afterPage, prefill.cursorPos)
109	    else focus.clear()
110	  }, [template])
111	
112	  const handleFileNameChange = useCallback((firstPage: number, name: string) => {
113	    setFileNames(prev => new Map(prev).set(firstPage, name))
114	  }, [])
115	
116	  return (
117	    <Box style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100%' }}>
118	      <Box
119	        style={{
120	          flexShrink: 0,
121	          borderBottom: '1px solid var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',
122	          display: 'flex',
123	          alignItems: 'center',
124	          paddingInline: 12,
125	          height: 44,
126	        }}
127	      >
128	        <Group gap={8} style={{ width: '100%' }}>
129	          <Button size="xs" variant="default" onClick={handleOpen}>
130	            Open PDF
131	          </Button>
132	          <TextInput
133	            size="xs"
134	            placeholder={DEFAULT_TEMPLATE}
135	            value={template}
136	            onChange={(e) => setTemplate(e.currentTarget.value)}
137	            leftSection={<span style={{ fontSize: 11, whiteSpace: 'nowrap', color: 'var(--mantine-color-dimmed)' }}>Template</span>}
138	            leftSectionWidth={60}
139	            style={{ flex: 1 }}
140	          />
141	          <Button size="xs" disabled={!pdfPath} loading={exporting} onClick={handleExport}>
142	            Export
143	          </Button>
144	        </Group>
145	      </Box>
146	
147	      <Box style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'hidden', display: 'flex' }}>
148	        {pdfPath ? (
149	          <>
150	            <ThumbnailPanel
151	              pdfPath={pdfPath}
152	              pageCount={pageCount}
153	              selectedPage={selectedPage}
154	              onSelectPage={setSelectedPage}
155	              splitPoints={splitPoints}
156	              onToggleSplitPoint={toggleSplitPoint}
157	              fileNames={fileNames}
158	              onFileNameChange={handleFileNameChange}
159	              focus={focus}
160	            />
161	            <DetailPanel
162	              pdfPath={pdfPath}
163	              pageNum={selectedPage}
164	              pageCount={pageCount}
165	              onNavigate={setSelectedPage}
166	            />
167	          </>
168	        ) : (
169	          <Box style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
170	            <Button onClick={handleOpen}>Open PDF</Button>
171	          </Box>
172	        )}
173	      </Box>
174	    </Box>
175	  )
176	}
177

handlePickFolderOverride = useCallback(async (firstPage: number) => {\n    const folder = await PickFolder()\n    if (folder) setFolderOverrides(prev => new Map(prev).set(firstPage, folder))\n  }, - **`app.go`**: `ExportSplit` now takes `outDirs []string` (one per output file) instead of a single `outDir`

- **State**: `outputFolder` (global) + `folderOverrides: Map<number, string>` (per-file); overrides are cleared when a split point is removed, and when a new file is opened

> DEVELOPER

move splitPoints, fileNames and folderOverrides into hook with a per outputfile data structure? sensible?

> AGENT

Yes, sensible. The main gain is that `toggleSplitPoint` currently coordinates three separate state updates — that's the fragility. A single `Map<number, { name: string; folderOverride?: string }>` keyed by `firstPage` becomes the source of truth, and `splitPoints` becomes derived from it (`new Set([...outputFiles.keys()].filter(k => k > 1).map(k => k - 1))`).

The tradeoff: `ThumbnailPanel` receives `splitPoints` today as a plain `Set` for gap rendering — you'd either derive it before passing down (simple) or change the prop to `outputFiles` and derive inside (more invasive). Deriving at the call site is the lighter path.

The hook interface would be something like:

```typescript
const files = useOutputFiles()
// files.toggle(afterPage, prefillName, prefillCursorPos)
// files.setName(firstPage, name)
// files.setFolderOverride(firstPage, folder)
// files.reset(firstPageName)
// files.splitPoints  ← derived Set<number>
// files.all          ← Map<number, OutputFile>
```

Worth doing — want me to go ahead?

> DEVELOPER

1	import { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react'
2	import { Box, Button, Group, TextInput } from '@mantine/core'
3	import { notifications } from '@mantine/notifications'
4	import ThumbnailPanel from './ThumbnailPanel'
5	import DetailPanel from '../DetailPanel'
6	import { OpenPDF, PageCount, PickFolder, ExportSplit } from '../../../wailsjs/go/main/App'
7	import { basename } from '../../utils'
8	
9	const DEFAULT_TEMPLATE = '{date} {name}'
10	
11	function applyTemplate(template: string): { value: string; cursorPos: number } {
12	  const date = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]
13	  const withDate = template.replace('{date}', date)
14	  const nameIdx = withDate.indexOf('{name}')
15	  if (nameIdx === -1) return { value: withDate, cursorPos: withDate.length }
16	  return { value: withDate.replace('{name}', ''), cursorPos: nameIdx }
17	}
18	
19	// Tracks which filename input should steal focus after a split point is added,
20	// and where the cursor should land within it. Cleared by the input itself once
21	// it has focused, so only the first render after the split point is added fires.
22	function usePendingFocus() {
23	  const [pendingFocus, setPendingFocus] = useState<{ afterPage: number; cursorPos: number } | null>(null)
24	  const request = useCallback((afterPage: number, cursorPos: number) => setPendingFocus({ afterPage, cursorPos }), [])
25	  const clear = useCallback(() => setPendingFocus(null), [])
26	  return { pendingFocus, request, clear }
27	}
28	
29	interface Props {
30	  initialPath?: string | null
31	}
32	
33	export default function SplitMode({ initialPath }: Props) {
34	  const [pdfPath, setPdfPath] = useState<string | null>(null)
35	  const [pageCount, setPageCount] = useState(0)
36	  const [selectedPage, setSelectedPage] = useState(1)
37	  const [splitPoints, setSplitPoints] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set())
38	  const [fileNames, setFileNames] = useState<Map<number, string>>(new Map([[1, '']]))
39	  const [outputFolder, setOutputFolder] = useState<string | null>(null)
40	  const [folderOverrides, setFolderOverrides] = useState<Map<number, string>>(new Map())
41	  const focus = usePendingFocus()
42	  const [template, setTemplate] = useState(DEFAULT_TEMPLATE)
43	  const [exporting, setExporting] = useState(false)
44	
45	  const splitPointsRef = useRef(splitPoints)
46	  splitPointsRef.current = splitPoints
47	
48	  const resetForFile = (count: number, path: string, tmpl: string) => {
49	    setPdfPath(path)
50	    setPageCount(count)
51	    setSelectedPage(1)
52	    setSplitPoints(new Set())
53	    setFileNames(new Map([[1, applyTemplate(tmpl).value]]))
54	    setFolderOverrides(new Map())
55	    focus.clear()
56	  }
57	
58	  useEffect(() => {
59	    if (!initialPath) return
60	    PageCount(initialPath)
61	      .then(count => resetForFile(count, initialPath, template))
62	      .catch(e => notifications.show({ title: 'Failed to open file', message: String(e), color: 'red' }))
63	  }, [initialPath])
64	
65	  const handleOpen = async () => {
66	    const path = await OpenPDF()
67	    if (!path) return
68	    try {
69	      const count = await PageCount(path)
70	      resetForFile(count, path, template)
71	    } catch (e) {
72	      notifications.show({ title: 'Failed to open file', message: String(e), color: 'red' })
73	    }
74	  }
75	
76	  const handlePickOutputFolder = async () => {
77	    const folder = await PickFolder()
78	    if (folder) setOutputFolder(folder)
79	  }
80	
81	  const handlePickFolderOverride = useCallback(async (firstPage: number) => {
82	    const folder = await PickFolder()
83	    if (folder) setFolderOverrides(prev => new Map(prev).set(firstPage, folder))
84	  }, [])
85	
86	  const handleExport = async () => {
87	    if (!pdfPath || !outputFolder) return
88	    setExporting(true)
89	    try {
90	      const sorted = [...splitPoints].sort((a, b) => a - b)
91	      const firstPages = [1, ...sorted.map(p => p + 1)]
92	      const outNames = firstPages.map(fp => fileNames.get(fp) ?? '')
93	      const outDirs = firstPages.map(fp => folderOverrides.get(fp) ?? outputFolder)
94	      await ExportSplit(pdfPath, sorted, outDirs, outNames)
95	      const fileCount = splitPoints.size + 1
96	      notifications.show({
97	        title: 'Export complete',
98	        message: `${fileCount} file${fileCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''} saved`,
99	        color: 'green',
100	      })
101	    } catch (e) {
102	      notifications.show({ title: 'Export failed', message: String(e), color: 'red' })
103	    } finally {
104	      setExporting(false)
105	    }
106	  }
107	
108	  const toggleSplitPoint = useCallback((afterPage: number) => {
109	    const isAdding = !splitPointsRef.current.has(afterPage)
110	    const prefill = isAdding ? applyTemplate(template) : null
111	    setSplitPoints(prev => {
112	      const next = new Set(prev)
113	      if (next.has(afterPage)) { next.delete(afterPage) } else { next.add(afterPage) }
114	      return next
115	    })
116	    setFileNames(prev => {
117	      const next = new Map(prev)
118	      if (prefill) { next.set(afterPage + 1, prefill.value) } else { next.delete(afterPage + 1) }
119	      return next
120	    })
121	    if (!isAdding) {
122	      setFolderOverrides(prev => {
123	        const next = new Map(prev)
124	        next.delete(afterPage + 1)
125	        return next
126	      })
127	    }
128	    if (prefill) focus.request(afterPage, prefill.cursorPos)
129	    else focus.clear()
130	  }, [template])
131	
132	  const handleFileNameChange = useCallback((firstPage: number, name: string) => {
133	    setFileNames(prev => new Map(prev).set(firstPage, name))
134	  }, [])
135	
136	  return (
137	    <Box style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100%' }}>
138	      <Box
139	        style={{
140	          flexShrink: 0,
141	          borderBottom: '1px solid var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',
142	          display: 'flex',
143	          alignItems: 'center',
144	          paddingInline: 12,
145	          height: 44,
146	        }}
147	      >
148	        <Group gap={8} style={{ width: '100%' }}>
149	          <Button size="xs" variant="default" onClick={handleOpen}>
150	            Open PDF
151	          </Button>
152	          <TextInput
153	            size="xs"
154	            placeholder={DEFAULT_TEMPLATE}
155	            value={template}
156	            onChange={(e) => setTemplate(e.currentTarget.value)}
157	            leftSection={<span style={{ fontSize: 11, whiteSpace: 'nowrap', color: 'var(--mantine-color-dimmed)' }}>Template</span>}
158	            leftSectionWidth={60}
159	            style={{ flex: 1 }}
160	          />
161	          <Button size="xs" variant="default" onClick={handlePickOutputFolder}>
162	            {outputFolder ? basename(outputFolder) : 'Output folder…'}
163	          </Button>
164	          <Button size="xs" disabled={!pdfPath || !outputFolder} loading={exporting} onClick={handleExport}>
165	            Export
166	          </Button>
167	        </Group>
168	      </Box>
169	
170	      <Box style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'hidden', display: 'flex' }}>
171	        {pdfPath ? (
172	          <>
173	            <ThumbnailPanel
174	              pdfPath={pdfPath}
175	              pageCount={pageCount}
176	              selectedPage={selectedPage}
177	              onSelectPage={setSelectedPage}
178	              splitPoints={splitPoints}
179	              onToggleSplitPoint={toggleSplitPoint}
180	              fileNames={fileNames}
181	              onFileNameChange={handleFileNameChange}
182	              outputFolder={outputFolder}
183	              folderOverrides={folderOverrides}
184	              onPickFolderOverride={handlePickFolderOverride}
185	              focus={focus}
186	            />
187	            <DetailPanel
188	              pdfPath={pdfPath}
189	              pageNum={selectedPage}
190	              pageCount={pageCount}
191	              onNavigate={setSelectedPage}
192	            />
193	          </>
194	        ) : (
195	          <Box style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
196	            <Button onClick={handleOpen}>Open PDF</Button>
197	          </Box>
198	        )}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ben/git/paper-scan-processor/frontend/src/components/SplitMode/useOutputFiles.ts",
  "content": "import { useState, useCallback, useRef } from 'react'\n\nexport interface OutputFile {\n  name: string\n  folderOverride?: string\n}\n\n// Keyed by firstPage of each output section. firstPage 1 is always present.\ntype OutputFilesMap = Map<number, OutputFile>\n\nfunction deriveSplitPoints(files: OutputFilesMap): Set<number> {\n  const result = new Set<number>()\n  for (const firstPage of files.keys()) {\n    if (firstPage > 1) result.add(firstPage - 1)\n  }\n  return result\n}\n\nexport interface OutputFilesHandle {\n  all: OutputFilesMap\n  splitPoints: Set<number>\n  toggle: (afterPage: number, prefillName: string) => void\n  setName: (firstPage: number, name: string) => void\n  setFolderOverride: (firstPage: number, folder: string) => void\n  reset: (firstPageName: string) => void\n}\n\nexport function useOutputFiles(): OutputFilesHandle {\n  const [files, setFiles] = useState<OutputFilesMap>(new Map([[1, { name: '' }]]))\n\n  // Ref so toggle can read current state without becoming stale across template changes\n  const filesRef = useRef(files)\n  filesRef.current = files\n\n  const toggle = useCallback((afterPage: number, prefillName: string) => {\n    setFiles(prev => {\n      const next = new Map(prev)\n      const firstPage = afterPage + 1\n      if (next.has(firstPage)) {\n        next.delete(firstPage)\n      } else {\n        next.set(firstPage, { name: prefillName })\n      }\n      return next\n    })\n  }, [])\n\n  const setName = useCallback((firstPage: number, name: string) => {\n    setFiles(prev => {\n      const entry = prev.get(firstPage)\n      if (!entry) return prev\n      return new Map(prev).set(firstPage, { ...entry, name })\n    })\n  }, [])\n\n  const setFolderOverride = useCallback((firstPage: number, folder: string) => {\n    setFiles(prev => {\n      const entry = prev.get(firstPage)\n      if (!entry) return prev\n      return new Map(prev).set(firstPage, { ...entry, folderOverride: folder })\n    })\n  }, [])\n\n  const reset = useCallback((firstPageName: string) => {\n    setFiles(new Map([[1, { name: firstPageName }]]))\n  }, [])\n\n  return {\n    all: files,\n    splitPoints: deriveSplitPoints(files),\n    toggle,\n    setName,\n    setFolderOverride,\n    reset,\n  }\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ben/git/paper-scan-processor/frontend/src/components/SplitMode/index.tsx",
  "content": "import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react'\nimport { Box, Button, Group, TextInput } from '@mantine/core'\nimport { notifications } from '@mantine/notifications'\nimport ThumbnailPanel from './ThumbnailPanel'\nimport DetailPanel from '../DetailPanel'\nimport { OpenPDF, PageCount, PickFolder, ExportSplit } from '../../../wailsjs/go/main/App'\nimport { basename } from '../../utils'\nimport { useOutputFiles } from './useOutputFiles'\n\nconst DEFAULT_TEMPLATE = '{date} {name}'\n\nfunction applyTemplate(template: string): { value: string; cursorPos: number } {\n  const date = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]\n  const withDate = template.replace('{date}', date)\n  const nameIdx = withDate.indexOf('{name}')\n  if (nameIdx === -1) return { value: withDate, cursorPos: withDate.length }\n  return { value: withDate.replace('{name}', ''), cursorPos: nameIdx }\n}\n\n// Tracks which filename input should steal focus after a split point is added,\n// and where the cursor should land within it. Cleared by the input itself once\n// it has focused, so only the first render after the split point is added fires.\nfunction usePendingFocus() {\n  const [pendingFocus, setPendingFocus] = useState<{ afterPage: number; cursorPos: number } | null>(null)\n  const request = useCallback((afterPage: number, cursorPos: number) => setPendingFocus({ afterPage, cursorPos }), [])\n  const clear = useCallback(() => setPendingFocus(null), [])\n  return { pendingFocus, request, clear }\n}\n\ninterface Props {\n  initialPath?: string | null\n}\n\nexport default function SplitMode({ initialPath }: Props) {\n  const [pdfPath, setPdfPath] = useState<string | null>(null)\n  const [pageCount, setPageCount] = useState(0)\n  const [selectedPage, setSelectedPage] = useState(1)\n  const [outputFolder, setOutputFolder] = useState<string | null>(null)\n  const outputFiles = useOutputFiles()\n  const focus = usePendingFocus()\n  const [template, setTemplate] = useState(DEFAULT_TEMPLATE)\n  const [exporting, setExporting] = useState(false)\n\n  const resetForFile = (count: number, path: string, tmpl: string) => {\n    setPdfPath(path)\n    setPageCount(count)\n    setSelectedPage(1)\n    outputFiles.reset(applyTemplate(tmpl).value)\n    focus.clear()\n  }\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!initialPath) return\n    PageCount(initialPath)\n      .then(count => resetForFile(count, initialPath, template))\n      .catch(e => notifications.show({ title: 'Failed to open file', message: String(e), color: 'red' }))\n  }, [initialPath])\n\n  const handleOpen = async () => {\n    const path = await OpenPDF()\n    if (!path) return\n    try {\n      const count = await PageCount(path)\n      resetForFile(count, path, template)\n    } catch (e) {\n      notifications.show({ title: 'Failed to open file', message: String(e), color: 'red' })\n    }\n  }\n\n  const handlePickOutputFolder = async () => {\n    const folder = await PickFolder()\n    if (folder) setOutputFolder(folder)\n  }\n\n  const handlePickFolderOverride = useCallback(async (firstPage: number) => {\n    const folder = await PickFolder()\n    if (folder) outputFiles.setFolderOverride(firstPage, folder)\n  }, [outputFiles.setFolderOverride])\n\n  const handleExport = async () => {\n    if (!pdfPath || !outputFolder) return\n    setExporting(true)\n    try {\n      const sorted = [...outputFiles.splitPoints].sort((a, b) => a - b)\n      const firstPages = [1, ...sorted.map(p => p + 1)]\n      const outNames = firstPages.map(fp => outputFiles.all.get(fp)?.name ?? '')\n      const outDirs = firstPages.map(fp => outputFiles.all.get(fp)?.folderOverride ?? outputFolder)\n      await ExportSplit(pdfPath, sorted, outDirs, outNames)\n      const fileCount = outputFiles.splitPoints.size + 1\n      notifications.show({\n        title: 'Export complete',\n        message: `${fileCount} file${fileCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''} saved`,\n        color: 'green',\n      })\n    } catch (e) {\n      notifications.show({ title: 'Export failed', message: String(e), color: 'red' })\n    } finally {\n      setExporting(false)\n    }\n  }\n\n  const handleToggleSplitPoint = useCallback((afterPage: number) => {\n    const isAdding = !outputFiles.splitPoints.has(afterPage)\n    const prefill = isAdding ? applyTemplate(template) : null\n    outputFiles.toggle(afterPage, prefill?.value ?? '')\n    if (prefill) focus.request(afterPage, prefill.cursorPos)\n    else focus.clear()\n  }, [template, outputFiles.splitPoints, outputFiles.toggle])\n\n  return (\n    <Box style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100%' }}>\n      <Box\n        style={{\n          flexShrink: 0,\n          borderBottom: '1px solid var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',\n          display: 'flex',\n          alignItems: 'center',\n          paddingInline: 12,\n          height: 44,\n        }}\n      >\n        <Group gap={8} style={{ width: '100%' }}>\n          <Button size=\"xs\" variant=\"default\" onClick={handleOpen}>\n            Open PDF\n          </Button>\n          <TextInput\n            size=\"xs\"\n            placeholder={DEFAULT_TEMPLATE}\n            value={template}\n            onChange={(e) => setTemplate(e.currentTarget.value)}\n            leftSection={<span style={{ fontSize: 11, whiteSpace: 'nowrap', color: 'var(--mantine-color-dimmed)' }}>Template</span>}\n            leftSectionWidth={60}\n            style={{ flex: 1 }}\n          />\n          <Button size=\"xs\" variant=\"default\" onClick={handlePickOutputFolder}>\n            {outputFolder ? basename(outputFolder) : 'Output folder…'}\n          </Button>\n          <Button size=\"xs\" disabled={!pdfPath || !outputFolder} loading={exporting} onClick={handleExport}>\n            Export\n          </Button>\n        </Group>\n      </Box>\n\n      <Box style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'hidden', display: 'flex' }}>\n        {pdfPath ? (\n          <>\n            <ThumbnailPanel\n              pdfPath={pdfPath}\n              pageCount={pageCount}\n              selectedPage={selectedPage}\n              onSelectPage={setSelectedPage}\n              splitPoints={outputFiles.splitPoints}\n              onToggleSplitPoint={handleToggleSplitPoint}\n              outputFiles={outputFiles.all}\n              onFileNameChange={outputFiles.setName}\n              outputFolder={outputFolder}\n              onPickFolderOverride={handlePickFolderOverride}\n              focus={focus}\n            />\n            <DetailPanel\n              pdfPath={pdfPath}\n              pageNum={selectedPage}\n              pageCount={pageCount}\n              onNavigate={setSelectedPage}\n            />\n          </>\n        ) : (\n          <Box style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>\n            <Button onClick={handleOpen}>Open PDF</Button>\n          </Box>\n        )}\n      </Box>\n    </Box>\n  )\n}\n"
}

1	import { useState, useCallback, useRef } from 'react'
2	
3	export interface OutputFile {
4	  name: string
5	  folderOverride?: string
6	}
7	
8	// Keyed by firstPage of each output section. firstPage 1 is always present.
9	type OutputFilesMap = Map<number, OutputFile>
10	
11	function deriveSplitPoints(files: OutputFilesMap): Set<number> {
12	  const result = new Set<number>()
13	  for (const firstPage of files.keys()) {
14	    if (firstPage > 1) result.add(firstPage - 1)
15	  }
16	  return result
17	}
18	
19	export interface OutputFilesHandle {
20	  all: OutputFilesMap
21	  getSplitPoints: () => Set<number>
22	  toggle: (afterPage: number, prefillName: string) => void
23	  setName: (firstPage: number, name: string) => void
24	  setFolderOverride: (firstPage: number, folder: string) => void
25	  reset: (firstPageName: string) => void
26	}
27	
28	export function useOutputFiles(): OutputFilesHandle {
29	  const [files, setFiles] = useState<OutputFilesMap>(new Map([[1, { name: '' }]]))
30	
31	  // Ref so toggle can read current state without becoming stale across template changes
32	  const filesRef = useRef(files)
33	  filesRef.current = files
34	
35	  const toggle = useCallback((afterPage: number, prefillName: string) => {
36	    setFiles(prev => {
37	      const next = new Map(prev)
38	      const firstPage = afterPage + 1
39	      if (next.has(firstPage)) {
40	        next.delete(firstPage)
41	      } else {
42	        next.set(firstPage, { name: prefillName })
43	      }
44	      return next
45	    })
46	  }, [])
47	
48	  const setName = useCallback((firstPage: number, name: string) => {
49	    setFiles(prev => {
50	      const entry = prev.get(firstPage)
51	      if (!entry) return prev
52	      return new Map(prev).set(firstPage, { ...entry, name })
53	    })
54	  }, [])
55	
56	  const setFolderOverride = useCallback((firstPage: number, folder: string) => {
57	    setFiles(prev => {
58	      const entry = prev.get(firstPage)
59	      if (!entry) return prev
60	      return new Map(prev).set(firstPage, { ...entry, folderOverride: folder })
61	    })
62	  }, [])
63	
64	  const reset = useCallback((firstPageName: string) => {
65	    setFiles(new Map([[1, { name: firstPageName }]]))
66	  }, [])
67	
68	  return {
69	    all: files,
70	    getSplitPoints: () => deriveSplitPoints(files),
71	    toggle,
72	    setName,
73	    setFolderOverride,
74	    reset,
75	  }
76	}
77

Would it make sense to move handling of `prefill` in handleToggleSplitPoint() into the hook?

> AGENT

Not really. The hook owns file data; `focus` is a separate UI concern — coupling them would make the hook harder to reason about in isolation. The `applyTemplate` call also depends on `template`, which lives outside the hook.

The one small improvement available: `toggle` could return whether it added or removed, so `handleToggleSplitPoint` doesn't need to call `getSplitPoints().has()` as a separate read before calling `toggle`. But that's minor.

> AGENT

Clean. Also note `applyTemplate` is now always called (not conditionally) — the prefill value is just ignored when removing, which is fine since it's cheap.

> DEVELOPER

Explain the commit `  // Ref so toggle can read current state without becoming stale across template changes`. How would the state become stale?

> AGENT

The comment is misleading — the staleness risk has nothing to do with `template` changes specifically.

`toggle` has `[]` deps, making it a stable function that captures `files` from the first render. Without the ref, `filesRef.current.has(firstPage)` would always read the initial `new Map([[1, { name: '' }]])`, so `adding` would return `true` even for split points that had already been added — meaning you could never remove one.

The ref keeps `toggle` stable (no deps) while still reading the current `files` value.

The `setFiles(prev => ...)` updater form is fine without a ref because React passes it the current state. Only the `adding` check — which runs *before* the state update — needs the ref.

The comment should just say: "Ref so `toggle` can read current state without needing `files` as a dependency."

> DEVELOPER

Removing split points also works without using the ref, by just using `files`

> AGENT

Two issues with the current state:

1. `filesRef` is now dead code — `toggle` never reads it, it should be removed.
2. `files` in `toggle`'s `useCallback([])` closure is stale — it always sees the initial empty map, so `files.has(firstPage)` is always `false`. Autofocus when *adding* a split point would be broken (toggle always returns `false`).

The fix is to drop `useCallback` from `toggle` so `files` is always the current value from the enclosing render:

> TOOL

> AGENT

The plan: move `PickFolder()` into the hook as `pickFolderOverride(firstPage)`, remove `handlePickFolderOverride` from `index.tsx`, update ThumbnailPanel to accept `onPickFolderOverride: (firstPage: number) => void` bound to `outputFiles.pickFolderOverride`, and have `OutputFileHeader` call `setFolderOverride` via its prop (which is now the hook's method directly).

Wait — re-reading the request: "call `outputFiles.setFolderOverride` directly in `OutputFileHeader`". That means `OutputFileHeader` should call `setFolderOverride(folder)` — with `PickFolder` happening inside the hook. So `OutputFileHeader` calls `PickFolder()` itself and passes the result to a `setFolderOverride(folder: string)` prop. PickFolder moves into `OutputFileHeader`, not the hook.

Actually the clearest reading: `PickFolder` moves into the hook's `pickFolderOverride`, and `OutputFileHeader` receives `onPickFolder: () => void` bound to `() => outputFiles.pickFolderOverride(firstPage)` — so it calls the hook method directly (not through an intermediate in `index.tsx`). The intermediate `handlePickFolderOverride` in `index.tsx` goes away.

13	type ListItem =
14	  | { type: 'header'; fileIndex: number; firstPage: number }
15	  | { type: 'page'; page: number }
16	
17	function buildItems(pageCount: number, splitPoints: Set<number>): ListItem[] {
18	  const result: ListItem[] = []
19	  let fileIndex = 0
20	  for (let page = 1; page <= pageCount; page++) {
21	    if (page === 1 || splitPoints.has(page - 1)) {
22	      result.push({ type: 'header', fileIndex: fileIndex++, firstPage: page })
23	    }
24	    result.push({ type: 'page', page })
25	  }
26	  return result
27	}

29	export interface PendingFocusHandle {
30	  pendingFocus: { afterPage: number; cursorPos: number } | null
31	  clear: () => void
32	}
33	
34	interface Props {
35	  pdfPath: string
36	  pageCount: number

38	  onSelectPage: (page: number) => void
39	  splitPoints: Set<number>
40	  onToggleSplitPoint: (afterPage: number) => void
41	  outputFiles: Map<number, OutputFile>
42	  onFileNameChange: (firstPage: number, name: string) => void
43	  outputFolder: string | null
44	  onPickFolderOverride: (firstPage: number) => void
45	  focus: PendingFocusHandle
46	}
47	
48	export default function SplitThumbnailPanel({
49	  pdfPath, pageCount, selectedPage, onSelectPage,
50	  splitPoints, onToggleSplitPoint,
51	  outputFiles, onFileNameChange,
52	  outputFolder, onPickFolderOverride,
53	  focus,
54	}: Props) {
55	  const [panelWidth, setPanelWidth] = useState(DEFAULT_WIDTH)
56	  const [hoveredGap, setHoveredGap] = useState<number | null>(null)
57	
58	  const thumbWidth = panelWidth - ITEM_PADDING * 2
59	  const thumbHeight = Math.round(thumbWidth * PAGE_ASPECT)
60	  const pageItemHeight = thumbHeight + LABEL_HEIGHT + ITEM_PADDING + GAP_HEIGHT
61	
62	  const items = useMemo(() => buildItems(pageCount, splitPoints), [pageCount, splitPoints])
63	  // Ref so the scroll effect can read the current list without depending on it
64	  // (we don't want to re-scroll every time a split point is toggled).
65	  const itemsRef = useRef(items)
66	  itemsRef.current = items
67	
68	  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
69	
70	  const virtualizer = useVirtualizer({
71	    count: items.length,
72	    getScrollElement: () => scrollRef.current,
73	    estimateSize: (index) => items[index]?.type === 'header' ? HEADER_HEIGHT : pageItemHeight,
74	    overscan: 3,
75	  })

> DEVELOPER

Using the ExportSplit API is awkward: splitAfter, outDirs and outNames need to be in sync but are separate arrays. Also, The length of splitAfter doesn't match outDirs and outNames. Would it make sense to pass a data structure similar outFileMap_

> DEVELOPER

Using the ExportSplit API is awkward: splitAfter, outDirs and outNames need to be in sync but are separate arrays. Also, The length of splitAfter doesn't match outDirs and outNames. Would it make sense to pass a data structure similar outFileMap?

> AGENT

Yes. A slice of structs would fix both issues — split points become implicit in `FirstPage`, the data is co-located and can't drift:

```go
type OutputFileSpec struct {
    FirstPage int
    Name      string
    OutDir    string
}

func (a *App) ExportSplit(inPath string, files []OutputFileSpec) error
```

Wails auto-generates the TypeScript type, so the frontend would build `[]OutputFileSpec` from `outputFiles.all` directly. The Go side derives `splitAfter` from `files[i].FirstPage - 1` for all entries where `FirstPage > 1`. Want me to go ahead?

> DEVELOPER

yes

An empty name falls back to \"output-N\".\n// splitAfter contains 1-indexed page numbers after which a new file begins.\nfunc (a *App) ExportSplit(inPath string, splitAfter []int, outDirs []string, outNames []string) error {\n\ttmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp(\"\", \"psp-split-*\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tdefer os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)\n\n\tpaths, err := splitPDF(inPath, splitAfter, tmpDir)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tfor i, src := range paths {\n\t\toutDir := \"\"\n\t\tif i < len(outDirs) {\n\t\t\toutDir = outDirs[i]\n\t\t}\n\t\tname := \"\"\n\t\tif i < len(outNames) {\n\t\t\tname = strings.TrimSpace(outNames[i])\n\t\t}\n\t\tif name == \"\" {\n\t\t\tname = fmt.Sprintf(\"output-%d\", i+1)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !strings.HasSuffix(strings.ToLower(name), \".pdf\") {\n\t\t\tname += \".pdf\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := copyFile(src, filepath.Join(outDir, name)); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t}",
  "new_string": "// OutputFileSpec describes one output file for ExportSplit.\ntype OutputFileSpec struct {\n\tFirstPage int    // 1-indexed; first entry must be 1\n\tName      string // filename without extension; falls back to \"output-N\" if empty\n\tOutDir    string // destination directory\n}\n\n// ExportSplit splits the PDF at inPath according to files (sorted by FirstPage)\n// and writes each segment to its OutDir using its Name.\nfunc (a *App) ExportSplit(inPath string, files []OutputFileSpec) error {\n\ttmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp(\"\", \"psp-split-*\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tdefer os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)\n\n\tsplitAfter := make([]int, 0, len(files)-1)\n\tfor _, f := range files {\n\t\tif f.FirstPage > 1 {\n\t\t\tsplitAfter = append(splitAfter, f.FirstPage-1)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tpaths, err := splitPDF(inPath, splitAfter, tmpDir)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tfor i, src := range paths {\n\t\tname := strings.TrimSpace(files[i].Name)\n\t\tif name == \"\" {\n\t\t\tname = fmt.Sprintf(\"output-%d\", i+1)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !strings.HasSuffix(strings.ToLower(name), \".pdf\") {\n\t\t\tname += \".pdf\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := copyFile(src, filepath.Join(files[i].OutDir, name)); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t}"
}

> AGENT

> DEVELOPER

Is it convention to name the fields in OutputFileSpec starting with an uppercase letter?

> AGENT

In Go, yes — uppercase is required for exported fields, and Wails needs them exported to serialize across the boundary. But on the TypeScript side `FirstPage` / `OutDir` look odd; JS convention would be camelCase.

You can get the best of both by adding `json` struct tags to the Go struct:

```go
type OutputFileSpec struct {
    FirstPage int    `json:"firstPage"`
    Name      string `json:"name"`
    OutDir    string `json:"outDir"`
}
```

Then the TypeScript models file and the frontend object literals would use `firstPage`, `name`, `outDir`. Want me to do that?
